### [1] [TorchDCM: A Unified PyTorch-Native Package for Discrete Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19231)
*Baichuan Mo,Zhengzhong Ricky You,Xiqun Michael Chen,Ruimin Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TorchDCM是一个开源的Python离散选择模型(DCM)估计包，将选择数据和模型规范编译成统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎，支持CPU/CUDA上的估计、推断、预测和结构化报告。它在7个估计包的45个合成案例对比中全部完成、运行最快，相比Biogeme和Apollo中位运行时间减少89.1%-99.7%，CUDA额外提供12-71倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模且模拟密集的离散选择模型(DCM)估计需要反复评估效用、概率、导数及模拟似然。现有DCM软件(如Biogeme和Apollo)具备成熟的经济计量工作流，但近期GPU导向工具仅加速部分模型，在计量覆盖范围与可扩展的微分计算之间存在缺口。

Method: TorchDCM将选择数据和模型规范编译为统一的PyTorch原生似然引擎。它涵盖Biogeme和Apollo的主要计量功能，包括多项、嵌套、混合、有序、潜变量和面板似然；支持不规则选择集、参数约束、协方差估计、支付意愿分析、弹性以及可扩展的似然组件。

Result: 在与7个估计包的对齐合成和真实数据全估计实验中，TorchDCM完成全部45个合成案例，在每个可比合成案例中运行最快，且在至少有两个可比解的所有对比中满足预设的最终对数似然容差。中位运行时间相比Biogeme和Apollo减少89.1%-99.7%，CUDA比单核TorchDCM额外提供12.0-71.0倍加速。

Conclusion: TorchDCM为计量经济估计和可微选择模型开发建立了可扩展且可复现的基础。其开源包和示例代码可在GitHub上获取。

Abstract: Estimating large and simulation-intensive discrete choice models (DCMs) requires repeated evaluation of utilities, probabilities, derivatives, and simulated likelihoods over many observations, alternatives, and draws. Existing DCM software provides mature econometric workflows, while recent GPU-oriented tools accelerate selected models, leaving a gap between econometric coverage and scalable differentiable computation. We introduce TorchDCM, an open Python package for discrete choice modeling that compiles choice data and model specifications into a unified PyTorch-native likelihood engine for estimation, inference, prediction, and structured reporting on CPU or CUDA devices. The package covers the principal econometric functionality available across Biogeme and Apollo, including multinomial, nested, mixed, ordered, latent-variable, and panel likelihoods. It also supports ragged choice sets, constrained parameters, covariance estimation, willingness-to-pay analysis, elasticities, and extensible likelihood components. We evaluate TorchDCM against seven other estimation packages in aligned synthetic and real-data full-estimation experiments. TorchDCM completes all 45 synthetic cases, runs fastest in every comparable synthetic case, and satisfies the prespecified final-log-likelihood tolerance in every comparison with at least two comparable solutions. More precisely, it reduces median runtime by 89.1%-99.7% relative to Biogeme and Apollo across model-data settings. CUDA provides an additional 12.0-71.0x speedup over single-core TorchDCM. These results establish a scalable and reproducible foundation for econometric estimation and differentiable choice-model development. The open-source package and executed examples are available at https://github.com/mbc96325/torchdcm.

</details>


### [2] [Discrete Diffusion Inference-Time Control with Nested Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20123)
*Lohithsai Yadala Chanchu,Hany Abdulsamad,Christian A. Naesseth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究离散扩散语言模型中的推理时控制问题，提出使用嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（NSMC）和全适配嵌套SMC（FA-NSMC）方法来进行Feynman-Kac引导采样，以在无需重新训练的情况下将采样引向序列级奖励。实验表明，这两种方法在毒性和流畅性引导任务上优于best-of-n采样和bootstrap SMC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于粒子的方法（如best-of-n采样和bootstrap SMC）在推理时控制中存在过乐观（overoptimism）和权重退化（weight degeneracy）问题，需要更好的方法来引导离散扩散语言模型生成满足序列级奖励的文本。

Method: 将嵌套序贯蒙特卡洛（NSMC）和全适配嵌套SMC（FA-NSMC）方法形式化应用于Feynman-Kac引导框架中，用于离散扩散语言模型中的推理时文本生成控制，并识别和修正了先前公式中导致最终估计有偏的错误。

Result: 在毒性和流畅性引导两个任务上的实验表明，NSMC和FA-NSMC方法在性能上一致优于best-of-n采样和bootstrap SMC基线方法。

Conclusion: 嵌套SMC方法能有效克服best-of-n采样的过度乐观问题和bootstrap SMC的权重退化问题，为离散扩散语言模型的推理时控制提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study inference-time control for text generation in discrete diffusion language models, where the goal is to steer sampling toward sequence-level rewards without retraining. Prior work in this domain has focused on particle-based methods such as best-of-$n$ sampling and bootstrap sequential Monte Carlo, which may suffer from overoptimism and weight degeneracy, respectively. We address these limitations using \emph{nested} sequential Monte Carlo methods. We formulate nested SMC (NSMC) and fully-adapted nested SMC (FA-NSMC) for Feynman--Kac steering, identifying and correcting errors in prior formulations that lead to biased final estimates. We evaluate these methods on toxicity and fluency steering tasks, showing that NSMC and FA-NSMC consistently outperform best-of-$n$ and bootstrap SMC.

</details>


### [3] [Transfer Learning in Nonparametric Regression with Deep ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20255)
*Junpeng Ren,Carlos Misael Madrid Padilla,Yanzhen Chen,Oscar Hernan Madrid Padilla*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个适用于多组非参数回归的通用迁移学习框架。该方法假设各组共享一个共同结构并带有组特有的加性偏移，采用两阶段偏移学习过程：第一阶段汇集所有组的数据估计总体均值函数，第二阶段估计各组的偏移量，最终通过加性组合得到组级估计器。作者为所提框架建立了L2误差上界，覆盖了广泛的非参数估计器类别；当使用深度ReLU网络实例化时，在层次组合模型下推导出显式收敛速度，证明能克服维数灾难。论文还考虑了实现正向迁移的条件（更简单的函数学习和通过跨组池化样本进行数据增强），并通过大量模拟和真实数据实验验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据通常被自然划分为多个相关但异质的组（如不同医院、不同地区的数据），传统上要么将所有数据池化忽略组间差异，要么分别独立建模各组分数据量不足而难以获得好的估计精度。本文旨在提出一个通用的迁移学习框架，在组间共享结构的同时保留组特有信息，从而提升每组非参数回归的估计精度。

Method: 提出两阶段偏移学习框架：(1) 第一阶段将所有组的数据池化，估计一个共同的总体均值函数；(2) 第二阶段对每组分别估计其相对于总体均值的加性偏移函数；(3) 最终组级估计器为总体均值函数与该组偏移函数之和。理论上为任意满足温和复杂度和噪声条件的非参数估计器建立了L2误差上界，并结合深度ReLU网络在层次组合模型下推导显式收敛率，同时分析了实现正向迁移的条件。

Result: 建立了所提通用框架的L2误差上界；针对深度ReLU网络实例化，在层次组合模型下获得了显式收敛速率，能够克服维数灾难；识别出实现正向迁移的条件，包括利用更简单的函数学习和跨组池化样本的数据增强；模拟实验和真实数据实验均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多组非参数回归迁移学习框架在理论上可行、通用性强，能有效利用组间共享信息提升各组估计精度，并克服高维情形下的维数灾难问题，为多组结构化数据的非参数学习提供了理论保障与实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper develops a general transfer learning framework for nonparametric regression with data consisting of multiple groups. Under the assumption that groups share a common structure along with group-specific deviations in additive form, the proposed method employs a two-stage offset learning procedure: the first stage pools data from all groups to estimate an overall mean function, and the second stage estimates offsets for each group, yielding final group-level estimators through additive combination. Upper bounds on the $\mathcal L_2$ error are established for the proposed framework, covering a broad class of nonparametric estimators under mild complexity and noise conditions. When instantiated with deep ReLU networks, explicit convergence rates are derived under hierarchical composition models, demonstrating the ability to overcome the curse of dimensionality. Conditions that enable positive transfer with faster rates are considered, including learning with simpler functions and data augmentation through pooling samples across groups. Various simulations and real-data experiments further validate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

### [4] [The structure of networks that evolve under a combination of growth, via node addition and random attachment, and contraction, via random node deletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2209.10027)
*Barak Budnick,Ofer Biham,Eytan Katzav*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对结合增长（节点添加与随机连接）与收缩（随机节点删除）的网络演化过程，给出了度分布演化的解析结果。通过主方程得到时间依赖度分布P_t(k)的闭式解，包含随时间衰减的初始项和渐近分布项。在纯增长情形下渐近分布呈指数分布；在混合情形下则呈泊松类分布。特别发现了网络整体收缩时存在两个不同的收敛区间（η在-1/3处存在临界行为）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的许多网络（如社交网络、生物网络等）并非仅经历增长过程，而是同时存在节点的加入和删除。此前网络演化理论大多聚焦于纯增长模型，对增长与收缩竞争过程下网络结构的解析刻画不足。作者旨在通过解析方法揭示增长-收缩平衡如何影响网络的度分布结构。

Method: 该模型在每个时间步以概率P_add执行节点添加和随机连接操作，以概率P_del执行随机节点删除操作，由参数η=P_add-P_del刻画增长与收缩的平衡。研究者利用主方程方法，推导出时间依赖度分布P_t(k)的闭式解析表达式，并分析不同η参数区间下P_t(k)的收敛行为及渐近分布P_st(k)的形式。

Result: 度分布P_t(k)包含一个依赖于初始度分布、随时间衰减的项，以及渐近分布P_st(k)。当η=1（纯增长）时P_st(k)为指数分布；当-1<η<1时P_st(k)为泊松类项的叠加并具有泊松类尾部。在整体收缩区间内，-1/3<η<0时P_t(k)快速收敛到P_st(k)；而-1<η<-1/3时收敛非常缓慢，仅在网络临近消亡前才接近P_st(k)。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了增长与收缩竞争过程中网络度分布的普适结构，并发现了η=-1/3这一临界点，其在整体收缩情形下划分了两种截然不同的收敛动态。这一结果为理解现实中经历节点更替的网络（如在线社交平台、组织网络等）的结构演化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present analytical results for the emerging structure of networks that evolve via a combination of growth (by node addition and random attachment) and contraction (by random node deletion). To this end we consider a network model in which at each time step a node addition and random attachment step takes place with probability $P_{add}$ and a random node deletion step takes place with probability $P_{del}=1-P_{add}$. The balance between the growth and contraction processes is captured by the parameter $η=P_{add}-P_{del}$. The case of pure network growth is described by $η=1$. In case that $0<η<1$ the rate of node addition exceeds the rate of node deletion and the overall process is of network growth. In the opposite case, where $-1<η<0$, the overall process is of network contraction, while in the special case of $η=0$ the expected size of the network remains fixed, apart from fluctuations. Using the master equation we obtain a closed form expression for the time dependent degree distribution $P_t(k)$. The degree distribution $P_t(k)$ includes a term that depends on the initial degree distribution $P_0(k)$, which decays as time evolves, and an asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of pure network growth ($η=1$) the asymptotic distribution $P_{st}(k)$ follows an exponential distribution, while for $-1<η<1$ it consists of a sum of Poisson-like terms and exhibits a Poisson-like tail. In the case of overall network growth ($0 < η< 1$) the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ eventually converges to $P_{st}(k)$. In the case of overall network contraction ($-1 < η< 0$) we identify two different regimes. For $-1/3 < η< 0$ the degree distribution $P_t(k)$ quickly converges towards $P_{st}(k)$. In contrast, for $-1 < η< -1/3$ the convergence of $P_t(k)$ is initially very slow and it gets closer to $P_{st}(k)$ only shortly before the network vanishes.

</details>


### [5] [Exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19274)
*Mei Tao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文给出了具有周期-自由边界条件和有限尺寸的二维矩形Ising模型边界行中两个自旋之间关联函数的精确表达式，并探讨了这些精确表达式的若干性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为二维有限尺寸Ising模型中边界行自旋关联函数提供精确的解析表达式，并验证其与热力学极限下已有结果的一致性，同时揭示Ising模型的基本性质（温度降低时出现长程有序）。

Method: 推导周期-自由边界条件下二维矩形Ising模型边界行两自旋关联函数的精确表达式；通过讨论参数L和N取极限的顺序对热力学极限下关联函数表达式的影响来分析长程有序的涌现；借助图示说明不同尺寸对关联函数的影响；并将所得表达式与已有热力学极限结果进行形式比较和证明。

Result: 获得了边界行两自旋关联函数的精确表达式，证明其在热力学极限下与已有研究者的表达式形式一致；揭示了热力学极限的收敛依赖于L和N两个参数取极限的顺序，并通过图示展示了尺寸效应对关联函数的影响。

Conclusion: 本文得到的精确表达式在热力学极限下与已有结果在形式上等价，清晰地展示了Ising模型中温度降低引发长程有序的基本性质。

Abstract: We present exact expressions of correlation functions between two spins in the boundary row of the two-dimensional rectangular Ising model with periodic-free boundary conditions and finite size. Some properties of exact expressions obtained are discussed. The fundamental property of the Ising model that the long range order emerge as the temperature decrease is shown clearly; expressions of the correlation functions in the thermodynamic limit varies depending on the order of taking limits of two parameters L and N; the impact of different sizes on correlation functions is illustrated with the aid of diagrams. Expressions of the correlation function discussed in this paper in the thermodynamic limit has been presented by previous researchers, and we prove that expressions obtained in this article is identical in form to expressions provided by previous researchers in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum Rényi-Jarzynski Equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19320)
*Benjamin Bobell,Mert Okyay,Rahul Nandkishore*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用资源理论方法推导了一个非破坏性的量子Jarzynski等式，该等式以任意浴可观测量的结果为条件，得到Rényi-Jarzynski等式，用于量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下偏离平衡的程度，并可作为量子最优控制中的可调代价函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的量子Jarzynski等式依赖于会破坏相干性的测量协议。作者希望发展一种不破坏相干性的量子Jarzynski等式，以更好地处理有限量子系统中浴的漂移问题，特别是在量子最优控制中需要最小化浴漂移的场景（如态制备和门设计）。

Method: 采用资源理论（resource-theoretic）方法，以任意浴可观测量的结果作为条件，推导出非破坏性的量子Jarzynski等式。通过Rényi k-散度（Rényi $k$-divergence）来量化有限浴在非绝热驱动下偏离平衡的程度，并将其作为量子最优控制的可调代价函数。使用玩具模型展示不同Rényi阶数k下的竞争最小值的转变。

Result: 推导出Rényi-Jarzynski等式，该等式将浴漂移与Rényi k-散度联系起来。在玩具模型中观察到在某个临界k值下存在竞争最小值之间的转变，说明Rényi阶数可以调节对浴分布不同区域的敏感度。此外，当驱动参数随浴能级变化时，最小化浴漂移需要产生系统-浴纠缠。

Conclusion: Rényi-Jarzynski等式为非破坏性量子涨落关系提供了新框架，可作为量子最优控制中抑制交叉干扰（cross-talk）的可调代价函数。Rényi阶数k提供了一种调控手段来调节对浴分布不同区域的敏感性，而系统-浴纠缠在特定驱动条件下成为最小化浴漂移的必要条件。

Abstract: The Jarzynski equality provides a strict link between nonequilibrium work and equilibrium free energy changes. Its typical quantum formulations, however, rely on measurement protocols that destroy coherence. In this Letter, we use the resource-theoretic approach to derive a non-destructive quantum Jarzynski equality conditioned on the outcomes of an arbitrary bath observable. This yields the Rényi-Jarzynski equality, which quantifies a finite bath's drift from equilibrium under a non-adiabatic drive via the Rényi $k$-divergence. We further demonstrate that the Rényi-Jarzynski equality provides a tunable cost function for quantum optimal control problems where minimizing bath drift is desired, such as state preparation and gate design, enabling the minimization of cross-talk in finite quantum systems. Our toy model exhibits a transition between competing minima for some critical value of $k$, illustrating how the Rényi order tunes sensitivity to different regions of a bath distribution. Strikingly, when drive parameters vary across bath energy levels, minimizing bath drift requires generating system-bath entanglement.

</details>


### [7] [Exact partition function of arithmetic Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19605)
*Anu Dhochak,Ken Kikuchi,Shrinit Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了算术Ising模型（AIM）精确配分函数的紧凑公式，在2×2系统中通过q-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数给出解析表达式，并推导了自由能和熵的显式形式，发现高温下熵增加，支持熵序的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为d维算术Ising模型提供精确、可解析处理的配分函数表达式，从而深入理解该模型的热力学行为，特别是验证高温区熵增现象是否支持熵序这一概念。

Method: 对2×2系统，利用q-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta函数将配分函数解析化，并基于此推导自由能和熵的显式表达式。

Result: 得到了2×2 AIM系统配分函数的紧凑解析公式，以及自由能和熵的显式形式；发现高温下熵增加，为熵序的存在提供了支持。

Conclusion: 算术Ising模型的精确配分函数可通过q特殊函数解析表达，且高温区的熵增行为印证了熵序的物理机制。

Abstract: We present a compact formula for the exact partition function of the $d$-dimensional arithmetic Ising model (AIM). For a $2\times2$ system, we express it analytically using the $q$-Hurwitz-Lerch zeta function and derive explicit forms for the free energy and entropy. Additionally, we find that the entropy increases at high temperatures, supporting the presence of entropic order.

</details>


### [8] [State convertibility and fluctuation theorems from a dynamical reference: majorization meets martingales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19391)
*Davide Cugini,Giacomo Guarnieri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出g(t)-majorization概念，将热力学中态可转换性的判据（majorization和thermo-majorization）统一并推广到任意、可能随时间变化的参考分布。通过引入对偶图像，将态可转换问题转化为一维凸序（convex-order）问题，证明转换可行当且仅当相对种群分布k_j(t)/g_j(t)由鞅（martingale）连接。在此基础上推导出参考相对熵产生的精确涨落定理，其平均违背通过χ²散度界来检验假设参考演化与真实参考演化的失配，提供了一种无需独立表征真实参考的模型无关诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: State convertibility represents a fundamental concept used to determine whether a transformation is possible given a specific set of resources. In this work, we firstly unify and extend these notions to an arbitrary and possibly time-dependent reference distribution $g(t)$, introducing the concept of $g(t)$-majorization; we then introduce a dual picture whereby state convertibility is turned into a one-dimensional convex-order problem, which allows us to demonstrate that a transition is admissible if and only if the associated real-valued distributions of relative populations $ k_j(t)/g_j(t)$ are connected by a martingale. Building on it, we then derive an exact fluctuation theorem for a reference-relative entropy production whose average violation certifies, through a $χ^{2}$-divergence bound, the mismatch between an assumed and the true reference evolution---a model-independent diagnostic that requires no independent characterization of the latter and turns an observed breakdown of the fluctuation relation into a certified lower bound on the reference error.

Abstract: We argue that the variance of the information content ($C$), an information-theoretic quantity, can be naturally interpreted as a measure of statistical complexity. This interpretation establishes direct connections with thermodynamics and phase transitions: for systems obeying Boltzmann--Gibbs statistics, $C$ is extensive and directly proportional to energy fluctuations and heat capacity. Moreover, unlike other statistical complexity measures, it attains a maximum at continuous phase transitions, as illustrated for the two-dimensional Ising model. Applications to chaotic maps and fractional Gaussian noise further indicate that $C$ captures nontrivial dynamical structure in different classes of correlated systems.

Abstract: We study the translocation of a polymer chain through a nanopore where the chain length fluctuates stochastically due to the polymerization-depolymerization processes at the chain ends. The translocation process terminates when the pore hits either of the two outer diffusing sinks. In the case where the pore motion itself is diffusive with diffusion constant $D_2$, we compute exactly the splitting probability that the pore hits the left (right) sink before hitting the right (left) sink. We show that the splitting probability in the presence of mobile sinks is rather nontrivial compared to the classical case of immobile sinks (the latter corresponds to the case when the chain length is fixed). Furthermore, we also compute exactly the probability distribution of the translocation time and that of the chain length at the completion time of the translocation. We show that both distributions have power law tails with exponents that depend continuously on the diffusion constants $D_1$, $D_2$ and $D_3$. We validate our analytical predictions via numerical simulations. We then present numerical results for the case when the pore performs a fractional Brownian motion with Hurst exponent $0<H<1$, while the sinks are still diffusive.

</details>

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations connect current fluctuations to dissipation and are often rooted in fluctuation-response principles. In underdamped dynamics, however, conventional mean-current uncertainty relations can fail, while the underlying connection between fluctuations, response, and dissipation remains elusive. Here we uncover this structure by deriving an exact finite-time fluctuation-response equality for underdamped Langevin dynamics, valid for arbitrary time-dependent driving and general additive observables. The equality yields sharp response bounds and a variational characterization of the dynamically generated variance. Choosing the perturbation along the irreversible probability flow gives a friction-response thermodynamic uncertainty relation that can be saturated at any finite observation time, leading to an exact variational principle for the total entropy production. We further show that the conventional uncertainty factor of velocity-resolved currents can decay exponentially with dissipation even in driven free diffusion, while the friction-response factor retains its universal lower bound. These results establish response, rather than the mean current itself, as the quantity directly linking fluctuations and dissipation in underdamped dynamics.

Abstract: We report asymmetric kinetics in thermally driven metal-insulator transitions (MITs) in 1T-TaS$_2$. Using combined transport, calorimetric, and Raman measurements, we show that the transition proceeds via burst-like avalanches during cooling, while remaining continuous during heating. Although bulk transport is masked by percolative conduction, local probes and thermal measurements reveal intrinsic asymmetry in the transformation pathways. Using controlled nonequilibrium thermal perturbations generated by pulsed Joule heating, we demonstrate that the phase-ordering dynamics remains strongly athermal during cooling, whereas during heating fluctuations progressively overcome nucleation barriers, leading to a smooth transformation. The distinct responses to thermal perturbations indicate different degrees of athermality of the two hysteresis branches, which govern the transformation pathways and give rise to the observed kinetic asymmetry. These results establish a general framework in which the degree of athermality controls pathway selection in first-order phase transitions.

Abstract: Global quenches at one-dimensional critical points admit a boundary conformal field theory (BCFT) description in which Euclidean strip correlators are analytically continued to real time. In the interacting Yang-Lee spin chain, statically calibrated boundary data determine the biorthogonal dynamics of the complete-character return amplitude, a primary one-point function, and a spatial correlator. Further results test this formalism with a direct field-on quench, mixed left and right preparations, and a complex five-state Potts fixed point with complex primary dimensions. Abstract: Motivated by a previous Ising study, we identify a ${\it higher}$ Nishimori line in the learning phase diagram of the $2D$ $q$-state Potts model $(2 < q\leq 4)$ under bond-energy measurements. Using extensive numerical tools, we confirm these statements for a generic, discrete $q$-state measurement protocol and determine precise numerical estimates for the location of higher and ordinary Nishimori critical points as well as RG flows between the various fixed points. We also discuss the Casimir effective central charges of the critical points in the learning phase diagram, and their monotonic ${\it decrease}$ along measurement-induced RG flows, as established non-perturbatively by the c-effective theorem and its extensions, and contrast it to the monotonic increase along the corresponding RG flows in the random-bond Potts model. Finally, we discuss a general argument based on ${\it Elitzur's \; theorem}$ that establishes stability of the ordinary Nishimori critical points in their respective learning phase diagrams. Equivalently, our results describe a monitored deformed $\mathbb{Z}_q$ toric code where the tricritical ${\it higher}$ Nishimori point is an 'information' critical point that separates stable quantum, classical, and no memory phases.

Abstract: Modern experiments often record nonequilibrium dynamics as sequences of discrete events whose rates depend on the realized past. Each transition count nevertheless obeys an exact stochastic equation. After the history-dependent mean event tendency is subtracted, the remaining random increment is a martingale increment--the part of the event that cannot be predicted from the past. Martingale increments associated with different transitions and different times are orthogonal. These increments form a complete orthogonal basis for the random deviation of any observable measured from the record, such as a current, occupation time, or event count. The coefficient of a given increment is the event-consequence kernel. It measures how that event changes the predicted final observable, relative to continuing without the event, for the particular history already realized. Multiplying this kernel by the event intensity gives the history-conditioned response to perturbing the corresponding transition rate. Thus, the intensity-normalized response is exactly the expansion coefficient of that event in the observable fluctuation. This identification yields exact finite-time and finite-frequency fluctuation-response relations. Averaging over histories leaves a nonnegative response-heterogeneity gap. The gap measures how strongly the consequence of the same event varies across histories and tests whether a proposed memory coordinate is response-sufficient. Finite-history versions can be estimated from spontaneous trajectories. Bounding the logarithmic rate sensitivity further gives response-kinetic uncertainty relations controlled by dynamical activity.

Conclusion: 本文为四波动能方程在非零热力学平衡附近的长期行为提供了严格的理论基础，填补了非零平衡附近全局适定性与渐近稳定性方面的重要空白。研究表明，即使在碰撞算子非紧的情况下，通过合适的加权L^2空间和精细的谱分析仍可获得指数松弛。该结果为更深层的波湍流动力学（如更一般的平衡、长时间行为与奇异谱情形）研究奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of the kinetic wave equation associated to the three dimensional Schrödinger equation close to Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria. We first prove that the linearised operator generates a semigroup of contractions in $L^2((0,\infty);\sqrt ω\ddω)$. Considering the family of nonsingular Rayleigh-Jeans spectra, we prove that the linearised operator possesses a spectral gap, despite the non-compactness of the integral collisional operator, and thus obtain an exponential relaxation for the linear semigroup. We then prove bilinear and trilinear estimates in the relevant norm for the nonlinear terms and deduce global well-posedness and exponential relaxation for sufficiently small relative perturbations of Rayleigh-Jeans equilibria. To our knowledge, this is the first global strong well-posedness and asymptotic stability result near a nonzero thermodynamic equilibrium for the full spatially homogeneous four-wave kinetic equation associated with the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation.

For second-order operators, this framework yields a finite characteristic system and nonlinear boundary equations, together with explicit solvable examples. For a third-order linear-potential operator, the additional Airy-flow identity permits a stronger sequential recovery: the first Heat--Airy compatibility equation determines \(f_s(t,0)\), and the next spatial-jet equation then reproduces the nonlinear compatibility condition obtained in the pure Heat theory from a \(4\times4\) characteristic determinant. Thus the additional commuting flow reorganizes the moving-boundary compatibility problem into a hierarchy while retaining the classical Heat theory as a distinguished restriction.

  \[

Abstract: This work presents a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for modeling a transient two-dimensional electrochemical flow reactor with diffusion, migration, convection, and nonlinear anodic Butler--Volmer kinetics. The model is trained without labeled concentration data by embedding the governing transport equation and all initial and boundary conditions into a composite loss function. Spatial and temporal coordinates together with anodic overpotential, temperature, inlet concentration, maximum flow velocity, and diffusivity are used as inputs, allowing the network to predict concentration fields over a broad operating domain. Validation against finite-difference-based solutions shows strong agreement for representative transient and near-steady cases, with a mean relative space--time error of $(9.99 \pm 0.65)\times10^{-3}$ (sub-percent level) across the conditioning domain. PINN inference is faster than a traditional finite difference solver by a factor of $5.34$, thus reducing runtime by $81.3\%$. Generalization tests further show that the surrogate remains robust under in-domain boundary-focused sampling and controlled extrapolation, although anodic overpotential is the most challenging parameter due to its exponential effect on interfacial kinetics. The results indicate that physics-informed neural networks can serve as accurate and efficient parametric surrogates for electrochemical transport problems and provide a foundation for low-computational-cost digital-twin modeling of electrochemical flow reactors.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the numerical computation of eigenfunctions of the Landau Hamiltonian with a periodic potential. Abstract: Resonant-state expansion (RSE) is a powerful modal framework for the perturbative analysis of open resonant systems, providing direct access to complex eigenfrequencies and eigenmodes. While RSE is well developed in electromagnetism, a comparably systematic formulation for acoustics remains less established. Here, we develop a general Green-function-based formalism for acoustic RSE and illustrate it for a class of two-dimensional acoustic resonators. Using the resonant states of an analytically solvable cylindrical reference system as a basis, we derive explicit perturbation matrix elements for uniform, radial, and sectoral variations of density and compressibility, representing homogeneous tuning, graded profiles, and symmetry-induced modal coupling. The resulting complex eigenfrequencies and eigenmodes are validated against exact analytical solutions and finite-element simulations, showing excellent quantitative agreement. The framework provides a systematic and physically transparent approach for analyzing perturbed open acoustic resonators and establishes a basis for resonant-state methods in acoustic metamaterials and non-Hermitian acoustics.

Abstract: We develop a resonant-state expansion formulation for acoustic scattering by individual resonators. The scattered pressure and particle-velocity fields are expanded over the resonant states of the system, with excitation amplitudes determined by overlap integrals between the incident field and the resonant states over the resonator volume. Using the acoustic energy flux, we derive expressions for the extinction, scattering, and absorption cross-sections and show that the extinction spectrum can be resolved into contributions from individual resonant states. The formulation is first validated for a homogeneous two-dimensional cylinder, where it reproduces the analytical Mie-theory solution. We then consider a sectorally perturbed cylinder with coupled azimuthal modes and demonstrate agreement with finite-element simulations. Finally, we combine the eigenvalue and scattering formulations for a material-programmed hard-wall annular metaatom and reproduce its scattering spectra and near fields. The developed framework provides a physically transparent modal approach to acoustic scattering by open resonators with reduced symmetry and spatially structured material parameters.

Conclusion: 本文为实值域上Banach代数范数的乘法性、谱性和谱乘法性在基域扩张下的保持问题提供了系统的理论刻画，其判据与经典代数几何中的概念具有深刻的类比关系，并显著推广了已有文献的结果。

Abstract: Universally (or geometrically) multiplicative norms on Banach algebras over complete non-archimedean fields were used by Berkovich in his works on non-archimedean geometry, and later they were studied in some detail by Poineau. In this paper, we perform a more thorough study of the question when multiplicativity, spectrality and spectral multiplicativity of norms on algebras over real valued fields are preserved by ground field extensions. We obtain precise criteria quite analogous to the classical theory of geometric irreducibility and reducedness. In particular, we generalize the results of Poineau in a few aspects.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征代数几何中，经典消解算法（如Hironaka方法）往往依赖特征零中数值不变量的单调下降性质，而正特征下此类不变量可能失效。作者旨在构造一种不依赖点态数值不变量单调性的对象级（object-level）消解框架，从而在正特征域上实现典范的强嵌入消解和主理想化。

Method: 该方法以标记Rees代数的微分-积分饱和为出发点，构造了全Hasse活性包、滤过系数立方体、半线性Frobenius-Hasse源以及普通容许爆发的字面变换数据。通过一个六行亏格演算，将局域问题归约到曲面、环面单项式、二项式及加性型四类程序；再通过全局神经上的干净中心组合序列化与下降，构建全局替换证书。该证书承载后继地址、已偿商、类型化轨迹、显示父节点、重开数据与终端真值，使完备状态在单一依赖良基序中经历严格的multiset替换，从而保证迭代终止并重建正规交叉严格变换。

Result: 作者证明了：配有该全局替换证书的完备状态在单一依赖良基序下具有严格multiset替换性质，因而迭代必然终止；终止后的穷竭状态可重建为一条与有序边界法向交叉的正则严格变换。此外，该证书可通过刚性生成、跨代无重置、完全wild容量控制、中心或类型化退出二选一、结构化余纤维、显示父节点分配及字面终端真值等七项条件在对象层面实现。

Conclusion: 该工作为正特征域上的典范强嵌入消解与主理想化提供了一种全新的对象级构造路径，以良基历史与替换证书替代数值不变量的单调性论证，理论上推广了消解问题的可处理性，并给出了完整的程序页面供研究使用。

Abstract: Let k be a perfect field of characteristic p>0. We introduce an object-level construction for canonical strong embedded resolution and principalization over k. The construction program replaces monotonicity of pointwise numerical invariants by a well-founded history of addressed comparison factors. Starting from the differential-integral saturation of a marked Rees algebra, we construct total-Hasse activity packets, filtered coefficient cubes, semilinear Frobenius-Hasse sources, and literal transform data for ordinary permissible blowups. A six-row defect calculus routes local problems to certified surface, toroidal-monomial, binomial, and additive-type procedures, after which clean centre portfolios are serialized and descended on a global nerve.
  The central structure is a global replacement certificate transporting successor addresses, paid quotients, typed traces, displayed parents, reopening data, and terminal truth across macroblocks. We prove that a complete state equipped with this certificate admits a strict multiset replacement in a single dependent well-founded order; hence the iteration terminates and the exhausted state reconstructs a regular strict transform having normal crossings with the ordered boundary. We further formulate an object-level realization of the certificate through rigid generation, cross-generation no-reset, complete wild-capacity control, a centre-or-typed-exit alternative, structured cofibres, displayed-parent allocation, and literal terminal truth. The program page is also available at website https://sites.google.com/view/positive-char-resolution

</details>


### [78] [The arithmetic de Rham stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20203)
*Junhui Qin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文定义了特征p域上概形X的算术de Rham栈X^{arith}，研究了其与Hyodo-Kato栈的关系，证明该栈为刚性上同调及其系数（超收敛isocrystals和算术D-模）提供了栈论方法，避免了先前方法中框架选择的长期问题，并给出了Berthelot关于光滑真推向前保持超收敛isocrystals猜想的证明等算术应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性上同调及其系数（超收敛isocrystals和算术D-模）的传统定义依赖于框架（frame）的选择，这一长期存在的框架选择问题使得理论形式化复杂且不自然。作者旨在通过栈论方法，即构造算术de Rham栈，为该理论提供一种不依赖框架选择的自然框架。

Method: 作者定义了特征p域上概形X的算术de Rham栈X^{arith}，这一构造受启发于de Rham栈（derived de Rham stack）理论。通过分析该栈与已有的Hyodo-Kato栈之间的关系，建立了它作为刚性上同调及超收敛isocrystals和算术D-模等系数的叠论（stack-theoretic）实现，从而绕过了传统方法中对框架选择的依赖。

Result: 论文证明了算术de Rham栈X^{arith}提供了刚性上同调及其系数的栈论框架，解决了框架选择的长期问题；并作为该形式体系的具体算术应用，证明了Berthelot猜想，即光滑真态射的推前保持超收敛isocrystals。

Conclusion: 算术de Rham栈为特征p域的刚性上同调理论提供了一种自然且无需框架选择的栈论基础，并成功应用于证明Berthelot猜想，表明该形式主义在算术几何中具有广泛的适用性和理论价值。

Abstract: We define and study the arithmetic de Rham stack $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ of a scheme $X$ over a field of characteristic $p$, and analyze its relation with related stacks such as the Hyodo--Kato stack. We show that $X^{\operatorname{arith}}$ gives a stack-theoretic approach to rigid cohomology and its coefficients, known as overconvergent isocrystals and arithmetic $D$-modules, which avoids the long-standing problem of frame-choosing in earlier approaches. We finally give some arithmetic applications of our formalism, such as a proof of Berthelot's conjecture on the preservation of overconvergent isocrystals by smooth proper pushforward.

</details>

### [79] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于监督式机器学习的新型直升机起飞重量估算模型，利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集进行训练，并按照EASA机器学习应用概念文件和Eurocae ED-324标准实施了学习保证流程。研究提出了一组机器学习需求、模型描述及其在LSTM循环神经网络上的实现，并在遗留航电计算机上验证了需求合规性，表明该模型可用于机载关键功能（如机上告警）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量是飞行安全和性能计算的关键参数，传统估算方法可能存在精度或实时性不足的问题。同时，航空领域对机器学习模型的适航认证和安全性要求日益严格，亟需一种符合监管框架（如EASA概念文件和ED-324标准）的监督式机器学习方法来提升起飞重量估算的准确性与可部署性。

Method: 采用监督式机器学习方法，使用空客全球在役机队的大规模运行数据集训练LSTM（长短期记忆）循环神经网络作为重量估算模型。研究构建了一套完整的机器学习需求（ML Requirements）和机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description），并遵循EASA机器学习应用概念文件及Eurocae ED-324标准框架执行学习保证流程，最终在遗留航电计算机上对需求实现进行验证。

Result: 成功实现了基于LSTM的直升机起飞重量估算模型，并在遗留型航电计算机上完成了验证，确认所提出的机器学习需求与实现方案相匹配。结果表明该实现适用于机载关键功能（如机上告警）的部署场景。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在航空适航认证框架下，通过系统化的学习保证流程可在遗留硬件上部署监督式机器学习模型用于关键飞行功能。这为机器学习模型在机载安全关键应用中的工程化落地提供了可行的技术路径和验证范例。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [80] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型模糊距离度量——三角模糊重标定距离（d_TR），将线性重标定直接融入距离计算中，解决了传统模糊距离度量在处理异构属性（不同尺度或单位）时需要预先归一化的问题。该度量被证明满足度量性质（非负性、同一性、对称性、三角不等式），并具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，适用于异构模糊数据的多准则决策、合成指标构建和基于距离的机器学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的决策常涉及不精确或不确定信息，通常用三角模糊数（TFN）表示。许多模糊距离度量假设所有值处于同一尺度，当应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需预先进行归一化处理，这增加了流程复杂性和潜在误差。因此，需要一种将归一化直接集成到距离计算中的度量方法。

Method: 提出三角模糊重标定距离（d_TR），将线性重标定（LRE）技术直接嵌入距离计算过程，在模糊数比较时同步完成归一化。通过严格的数学证明验证其满足度量的四项性质，并结合权重向量以优先考虑不同维度的重要性。

Result: 正式证明了d_TR满足非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式等度量性质，并进一步证明了该度量具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。

Conclusion: d_TR凭借其内置的归一化机制和优良的数学性质，非常适合处理异构模糊数据的应用场景，包括合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [81] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Holtercare-23K动态心电图文多模态数据集和Holtercare-Bench基准，用于评估和提升多模态大语言模型在长时段心电图（ECG）上的时间推理与诊断报告生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医学应用中主要处理静态图像或短期信号，在动态心电图这一关键领域，由于缺乏高质量数据集和基准，模型难以进行复杂的时间推理和诊断报告生成。

Method: （1）构建Holtercare-23K数据集，包含从788份临床Holter记录中提取的22,980个QA对，创新性地提出信号-视频-文本三模态对齐机制；（2）基于该数据集构建Holtercare-Bench多模态基准，从时间定位、临床诊断和全局总结三个维度评估模型。

Result: 零样本评测显示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列上存在显著性能差距；但对代表性模型进行微调后可获得大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLMs在电生理学领域的局限性，并为长期医学多模态大语言模型提供了基础性基准。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [82] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用标注数据集来增强LLM不确定性量化（UQ）的方法：通过构建简单分类器，将已有的UQ分数与相似查询的正确性作为特征来预测LLM响应是否正确，在计算开销极小的前提下持续优于现有的零样本UQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全部署离不开不确定性量化（UQ），但现有的UQ方法（如言语化置信度、多次生成）多为零样本方法，未利用部署前可获取的标注数据集。实际上在实际应用中，部署前总需要评估UQ方法在目标数据集上的表现，因此如何利用这些数据来提升UQ性能是一个值得探索的问题。

Method: 作者通过利用标注数据集，构建简单的分类器来预测LLM响应的正确性。分类器的特征包括两部分：已有的UQ分数（如言语化置信度、多次生成产生的分数等）以及相似查询的正确性信息。该方法无需修改LLM本身，仅作为现有UQ分数的增强层。

Result: 该方法在各种已有UQ分数的基础上持续取得更优的性能（优于零样本基线），同时仅引入最小的计算开销，适合真实世界的部署场景。

Conclusion: 通过利用部署前可获得的标注数据集构建简单分类器，可以显著增强LLM的不确定性量化性能，这是一种廉价且直接的改进方式，对LLM在真实应用中的安全部署具有重要价值。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [83] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法用于基于cfDNA的肺癌检测，使用DNA片段组学和DNA甲基化特征，通过角度/密集角度特征映射编码到量子希尔伯特空间并构建保真度量子核，与经典SVM基线对比，发现量子核模型在片段组学数据上可提升AUC，在甲基化数据上性能与经典模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量胸部CT肺癌筛查虽可降低死亡率，但受限于筛查接受度、依从性和管理挑战。血液cfDNA生物标志物作为补充手段，因肺癌异质性及高维非线性分子信号特征，早期检测仍然困难，需探索更强大的机器学习方法以提升检测性能。

Method: 使用量子-经典混合机器学习框架：先进行特征选择得到20和40特征子集；将特征通过角度和密集角度特征映射编码到量子希尔伯特空间，采用多种纠缠策略；用精确态矢量模拟计算保真度量子核，与预计算核SVM及核PCA逻辑回归集成，并以原始特征训练的SVM作为基线对比。

Result: 跨多次留出评估中，量子核模型在两个数据集上均取得有竞争力的性能。片段组学数据上，多个20特征配置相对经典SVM基线提升了AUC，表明有效捕获了cfDNA片段的非线性结构；甲基化数据上经典SVM取得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且在部分情况下提升了特异性。特征数从20增至40并未持续提升性能且常增加变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法可作为cfDNA肺癌检测的有前景途径，尤其在片段组学数据中能有效建模非线性信号，支持进一步探索量子机器学习在液体活检领域的应用。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [84] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个确定性算法，能够精确计算任意二维奇异模型的局部实对数正则阈值（RLCT），其复杂度有界，适用于包括多项式神经网络在内的广泛模型类别，为基于采样的估计器提供基准真相，并在浅层场景下速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则如BIC在奇异模型（如深度学习）中因正则性假设失效而导致错误模型选择。WBIC依赖局部学习系数λ（解析情形下等于KL散度的局部RLCT），但精确计算学习系数仅限于特殊情况，一般仅能用基于采样的估计方法，缺乏确定性精确算法。

Method: 作者提出了首个确定性算法，对任意二维模型（其KL距离与多项式接触等价）精确计算局部RLCT。算法基于接触等价理论，并推导了其复杂度上界。

Result: 该算法在广泛模型类别上证明有效，应用涵盖多项式神经网络。精确计算不仅为采样估计器提供基准真值（ground truth），还揭示了采样方法无法察觉的学习系数的代数结构，且在浅层（shallow）场景下速度优于采样方法。

Conclusion: 这是首次实现对二维奇异模型局部RLCT的精确确定性计算，弥补了该领域仅依赖采样估计的空白，兼具理论价值（代数结构洞察）和实践价值（更快、更准）。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [85] [Green BOA: Determining the environmental break-even point for ML-based data compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19994)
*Caterina Doglioni,Akshat Gupta,Thomas Elliott,Hanzila Hussain,Sanjiban Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文总结了曼彻斯特大学两个暑期实习项目的成果，聚焦于基于机器学习的数据压缩算法在环境可持续性方面的收支平衡点，通过对比ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳当量与减少磁盘存储带来的碳当量节省，讨论其盈亏平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习在数据压缩领域的应用越来越广泛，需要评估其环境成本与收益，确定ML-based压缩算法在何时（即什么条件下）能够实现环境可持续性的收支平衡。

Method: 以ML-based无损压缩算法为例，分别估算ML训练和推理所需基础设施的碳当量排放，以及因减少磁盘存储需求而节省的碳当量，通过对比这两方面的碳当量来讨论收支平衡点。

Result: 通过碳当量估算与对比分析，得出了ML训练和推理成本与磁盘存储节省之间的收支平衡条件。

Conclusion: ML-based数据压缩算法需要达到一定的使用规模或条件才能实现环境可持续性的收支平衡，这为评估和优化此类算法的环境效益提供了量化依据。

Abstract: We summarise the outcome of two summer internship projects based at the University of Manchester, focused on the break-even point in terms of environmental sustainability for ML-based data compression algorithms. Using the example of a ML-based lossless compression algorithm, we compare estimates for the carbon-equivalent of the infrastructure needed for ML training and inference with the carbon-equivalent savings from reduced disk storage requirements, and discuss their break-even point.

</details>


### [86] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMomentum，一种将方向感知引入动量更新规则的优化器组件。不同于传统EMA动量对所有方向以固定速率遗忘，DeltaMomentum利用线性层梯度的key-value结构，通过delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向的遗忘速率由该方向出现的频率决定。作为drop-in替换可嵌入任意优化器，在多项任务上显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化器（如AdamW）使用EMA作为动量，对所有梯度方向以固定遗忘率处理。然而深度网络训练输入高度各向异性：少数方向被频繁查询，多数方向很少出现。现有方法通过在外部包装额外处理来应对各向异性，但动量更新规则本身未变。作者希望将方向感知直接构建到动量更新规则中。

Method: 核心观察：线性层梯度可分解为输入（作为key）和输出侧误差（作为value）的乘积。利用这一key-value结构，DeltaMomentum通过规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲，使每个方向的遗忘率由其出现频率决定。理论证明：它满足动量有效性、无需矩阵逆运算即可施加输入侧曲率校正，且比EMA更快清除固定和漂移最优下的陈旧方向。

Result: DeltaMomentum是任意优化器动量缓冲的drop-in替换，系数在μP下可跨宽度迁移，额外计算开销仅为gated-MLP块线性成本的22.2%-25.0%，无持久内存。在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW比AdamW在67M规模下最多节省46.39±4.32%步数、370M规模下节省22.12±0.80%步数（三个种子），1B Chinchilla最优预算下增益持续。Muon基线在同一协议下表现比DeltaAdamW差。SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny在CIFAR-10上增益同样成立。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum通过将方向感知内建到动量更新规则中，有效解决了深度训练中输入各向异性带来的优化难题，在多个模型规模和任务上显著优于传统EMA动量，且作为即插即用组件兼容现有优化器。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [87] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何的流形视角研究复杂参数化网络的损失景观，将参数空间视为Kähler流形，并利用自然梯度下降在复几何框架下分析训练动力学。作者引入Calabi-Yau信息流形来刻画病态曲率条件下的景观，并探讨负曲率（截面曲率、Ricci曲率）如何破坏损失景观，连接了几何分析与深度学习理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上深度学习的损失景观分析多基于实欧氏几何，但复杂参数化网络（如复值网络）的参数空间具有天然的复几何结构。作者希望利用Kähler几何与信息论流形视角，为这类网络提供更本质的景观分析工具，并解释曲率负定性与网络训练失败模式之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用信息论流形视角处理复杂参数化网络，利用Wirtinger Hessian在log-likelihood势上定义交叉熵下的Kähler信息度量；采用自然梯度下降更新规则，以逆度量缩放微分损失，使下降路径保持在全纯切丛中。引入Calabi-Yau信息流形，在非紧致设置下用全局势定义度量，通过楔积处处非零的全纯形式作为Kähler形式的外积（至多差常数倍），实现常行列式条件；在固定行列式下分析近似低秩度量的爆破效应，并研究负截面曲率与负定Ricci曲率对损失景观的破坏机制。

Result: 确立了复杂参数化网络的Kähler信息度量框架，证明了自然梯度下降在复几何设定下保持全纯切丛中的轨迹；在Calabi-Yau信息流形中，常行列式条件下的近似低秩度量会导致损失景观的爆破效应；负截面曲率（进而负定Ricci曲率）会破坏损失景观的良性结构，与神经网络在消失或负Ricci曲率下的保证失效模式相关联。

Conclusion: 本文通过Dolbeault渐近分析和Calabi-Yau信息流形为我们理解复杂参数化网络的损失景观提供了新的几何视角，揭示曲率特性（尤其是负曲率）在神经网络训练失败中的关键作用，在几何分析与深度学习理论之间建立了桥梁。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [88] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制断层扫描"（mechanistic tomography）框架，将patching、梯度、Hessian-vector product和子集干预等机械可解释性测量方法统一为y=Ax+w的共享测量结构，并给出从低成本测量出发、在预留干预上测试、校正失配、按需扩展测量族的实用流程。在HMM控制、Tracr和GPT-2-small IOI及Qwen-2.5-7B等场景中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性研究旨在获取模型未直接暴露的量（如表征状态、组件效应、交互、干预响应），但现有方法（patching、梯度、Hessian-vector product、子集干预）在不同访问假设下测量不同目标量，缺乏统一的数学结构描述，导致研究者难以系统选择测量方法并评估其充分性。

Method: 将测量过程统一形式化为y=Ax+w：A描述干预家族，x为待恢复的目标映射，w包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配。在此基础上提出实用流程：从最低成本的测量开始，在留出干预上按目标规模进行检验，校准简单失配，当出现结构化残差时扩展测量家族。用线性测量的稀疏聚合、梯度访问的有限探针、提升测量与Hessian-vector product等手段恢复映射。

Result: 在双HMM模型中，控制误差随观测器误差上升，目标改进可能掩盖干扰态移动；前向-only访问下稀疏聚合测量比坐标patching用更少干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改善局部归因映射；提升测量和HVP可恢复一阶映射错过的交互（Tracr证明所需家族取决于基）；GPT-2-small IOI中Name Mover-Negative Name Mover交互是三个跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B中有限校正使加性拒绝响应映射足够，留出误差不支持对提升的需要。

Conclusion: 机制断层扫描提供了一个统一的测量框架，使研究者能够根据访问假设和有证据的残差结构系统地选择和扩展测量家族，避免过度拟合或欠拟合目标机制。控制任务可作为严格验证场景，因为引导干预的估计充当观测器，其误差直接反映在控制性能上。是否需要进行二阶（交互）测量应基于留出误差的证据而非先验假设。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [89] [Time-Uniform Self-Normalized Concentration for Discounted Least Squares: Limits and Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19643)
*Yi-Shan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出强化学习/bandit分析中广泛使用的加权自归一化浓度不等式存在缺陷：对于折扣最小二乘估计器在非平稳问题中的时间一致保证，作者通过一个标量高斯反例证明其声称的有界半径以概率1被跨越。作者进一步给出了任何确定性任意时刻边界在该模型类上必须满足的下界（至少R√log(T/δ)量级），识别了证明中的错误根源，并证明了该加权不等式在固定确定性时刻仍然成立，同时提供了有限与无限时域的修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自归一化浓度不等式是bandit和强化学习分析中的标准工具，其加权扩展版本被广泛用于非平稳问题中折扣最小二乘估计器的时间一致保证。然而作者发现该不等式存在缺陷，因此需要纠正这一广泛使用的工具，避免下游分析基于错误的不等式产生无效结论。

Method: 作者通过构造一个固定参数的标量高斯反例来展示该不等式被违反；随后通过分析不同终端时间使用不同高斯混合分布导致固定时间混合不能构成一个超鞅这一关键问题，识别出证明中的逻辑错误；并证明在任意确定性时刻该加权不等式仍然有效，同时推导出有限和无限时域的修正版本。

Result: （1）构造了标量高斯反例，证明加权不等式声称的边界以概率1被跨越；（2）证明在δ≤1/2且T/δ足够大时，任何在该条件次高斯模型类上有效的确定性任意时刻边界在某个时间点必须至少为R√log(T/δ)量级；（3）识别出证明错误源于不同终端时间使用不同高斯混合分布导致无法构成统一超鞅；（4）证明加权不等式在固定确定性时刻仍然有效，并给出修正的有限与无限时域边界。

Conclusion: 该加权自归一化浓度不等式的时间一致版本是错误的，不能用于非平稳问题中折扣最小二乘估计器的统一保证；但固定时刻版本仍然有效，研究者应使用作者提供的修正版本，并需重新审视依赖该不等式的下游分析结论。

Abstract: Self-normalized concentration inequalities are standard tools in bandit and reinforcement-learning analyses. A widely used weighted extension claims an analogous time-uniform guarantee for discounted least-squares estimators in non-stationary problems. A simple scalar Gaussian counterexample with a fixed parameter shows that the claimed bounded radius is crossed with probability one. For fixed discount and regularization parameters, we further show that, when $δ\leq1/2$ and $T/δ$ is sufficiently large, any deterministic anytime boundary valid uniformly over the stated conditionally sub-Gaussian model class must be at least of order $R\sqrt{\log(T/δ)}$ at some time by horizon $T$; for nondecreasing boundaries, this order is required at time $T$. We identify the proof error: different terminal times use different Gaussian mixing distributions, so the fixed-time mixtures do not form one supermartingale, and the stopping-time argument does not repair this failure. Finally, we show that the weighted inequality remains valid at each fixed deterministic time, give valid finite- and infinite-horizon corrections, and discuss consequences for downstream analyses.

</details>


### [90] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于模板的神经网络鲁棒性验证加速技术进行了系统性研究，揭示了模板包含率在不同场景下的巨大差异，提出"联合稳定神经元"新指标解释这种差异，并设计了FastCert技术——自动跨越网络层分配模板、在无加速潜力时完全弃用模板，最终在基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上平均获得1.13倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在关键领域的广泛应用使得其鲁棒性验证日益重要。虽然已有研究表明证明共享（通过复用中间层抽象状态即模板）可以加速不完备验证技术，但模板加速在不同网络架构、属性、数据集和训练方法下的鲁棒性仍不清楚，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 首先进行系统性研究，分析模板加速在不同场景下的有效性边界；提出"联合稳定神经元"（jointly stable neurons）这一新指标解释模板包含率的变化；在此基础上设计FastCert技术，自动将模板分布到网络各层以提升性能影响，并在模板不太可能产生加速时完全放弃使用模板。

Result: 研究显示模板包含率在不同场景下差异很大，联合稳定神经元指标能够解释这种变化，表明某些情况下模板技术几乎不可能提供任何加速。FastCert在大量基于覆盖设计的L0验证任务上，相比现有基于模板的复用技术平均获得1.13倍加速。

Conclusion: 模板加速的有效性高度依赖具体场景，联合稳定神经元可作为判断模板是否有用的预测指标；FastCert通过智能选择分配模板的位置并在必要时放弃模板，能够在多样化场景下稳定地提供性能改进。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [91] [Complementary, Not Cumulative: Interaction Effects in Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Navier-Stokes Vortex Shedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19632)
*Devesh Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了PINN训练技巧在非定常圆柱绕流基准（DFG/Schäfer-Turek）上的组合效果，发现几乎所有技巧单独使用均不及基线，但将SIREN周期性激活与因果加权结合可解锁此前无法达到的精度（相对L2误差4.1%），而继续叠加其他技巧反而导致灾难性性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PINN被寄予厚望作为CFD求解器的替代方案，且已有大量改进训练技巧被提出，但这些技巧通常在单一场景下独立验证，缺乏对其组合效果（composability）的系统研究，开放问题在于这些技巧能否真正叠加生效。

Method: 在DFG/Schäfer-Turek非定常圆柱绕流基准上，系统性地将多种已知PINN训练技巧（包括SIREN周期性激活、因果加权等）逐一和组合进行对比实验，以OpenFOAM解为参考，评估平均相对L2误差。

Result: 单独使用时几乎所有技巧都不优于未处理基线；SIREN激活与因果加权的组合解锁了此前不可达的训练区域，将速度和压力场重建误差降至4.1%平均相对L2误差；在此基础上再添加其他技巧会导致灾难性的性能退化。

Conclusion: PINN训练干预之间存在非线性交互作用，单独有效的技巧组合后可能适得其反；更复杂的训练配方并不必然更好，提示研究者应系统评估技巧组合而非孤立验证。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) embed governing partial differential equations directly into the training loss, offering a promising alternative to costly CFD solvers for unsteady flows. Yet the growing list of techniques proposed to improve PINN training is typically validated one at a time, leaving open whether these techniques actually compose. We study this question in depth on the DFG/Schafer-Turek unsteady cylinder wake benchmark. In isolation, nearly every technique performs no better than an untreated baseline. However, combining periodic (SIREN) activations with causal weighting unlocks a previously inaccessible regime, reconstructing velocity and pressure fields to within 4.1% average relative L2 error against an OpenFOAM reference solution. Adding further techniques instead causes catastrophic performance degradation, demonstrating that individually effective PINN interventions can interact nonlinearly and that more elaborate training recipes are not necessarily better.

</details>


### [92] [Finite-Horizon Input-Output Dynamics of Minibatch Perturbations in AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19762)
*Kang Liu,Suyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdamW优化器中少量样本（minibatch）对未来训练损失的延迟影响。作者将AdamW建模为有限时域输入-状态-输出（ISO）动态系统，通过对联合动力学进行线性化，推导出一个有符号响应算子，将局部梯度扰动映射到其未来损失效应上，从而揭示优化器记忆如何塑造这些延迟影响的幅度、时间与符号。论文还推导了精确的多步误差分解，并在局部光滑性和受控激活切换条件下建立了有限时域的一阶精度保证。实验验证了响应机制与优化器状态效应，并通过重复未来分析揭示了延迟影响中可被ISO近似部分恢复的前瞻性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW优化器将过去的梯度信息存储在其优化器状态（一阶矩和二阶矩估计）中，因此单次minibatch更新不仅影响当前步骤，还会通过优化器记忆对后续训练产生延迟影响。然而，这种延迟效应此前未被系统性地建模与分析，理解其机理对于优化算法设计、超参数调整以及训练行为解释具有重要意义。

Method: (1) 将AdamW形式化为有限时域输入-状态-输出（ISO）系统，状态包含模型参数、一阶矩估计和二阶矩估计；(2) 对联合动力学进行线性化，推导出有符号响应算子，将局部梯度扰动映射到未来损失效应；(3) 推导精确的多步误差分解，并在局部光滑性与受控激活切换假设下建立一阶有限时域精度保证；(4) 通过成对轨迹（仅在一个梯度更新上不同，后续训练序列完全相同）进行实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的响应机制及优化器状态对延迟影响的效应。重复未来分析揭示了延迟影响中存在大量前瞻性（prospective）结构，且这些结构可以通过ISO近似部分恢复。代码已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: AdamW中的优化器记忆（矩估计状态）对样本的延迟影响具有可建模的结构化机制。通过ISO系统线性化可以准确刻画梯度扰动到未来损失的响应，且该延迟影响中蕴含的前瞻性结构可被近似恢复，为理解AdamW类优化器的训练动态提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: A minibatch can influence training beyond the update at which it is observed because AdamW stores past gradient information in its optimizer states. We study this delayed effect through paired trajectories that differ only in one gradient update and share the same subsequent training sequence. We formulate AdamW as a finite-horizon input--state--output (ISO) system whose state contains the model parameters and first- and second-moment estimates. Linearizing the joint dynamics yields a signed response operator that maps a localized gradient perturbation to its future loss effects, revealing how optimizer memory shapes their magnitude, timing, and sign. We further derive an exact multistep error decomposition and establish first-order finite-horizon accuracy under local smoothness and controlled activation switching. Experiments validate the response mechanism and optimizer-state effects, while repeated-future analyses reveal substantial prospective structure in delayed influence that can be partially recovered from ISO approximations. Code is available at https://github.com/Kanyooo/Loss_ISO.

</details>


### [93] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP)，一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架，通过将纵向弥散张量成像（DTI）与弱临床监督相结合，将阿尔茨海默病严重程度建模为低维概率潜在变量，并推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)以连续量化个体在疾病连续谱上的位置及其不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病本质上是一个连续的生物学进程，但现有基于神经影像的人工智能方法大多局限于基于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法捕捉疾病的纵向连续演变特性。

Method: DCP采用纵向贝叶斯学习框架，将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜在变量，通过联合整合纵向观察数据与弱临床监督进行推断，并由此推导出Disease Continuum Score (DCS)以量化个体在AD连续谱上的位置及其关联的不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明，DCP在多个代表性疾病进展方法中持续取得最优性能。综合验证分析显示，DCS能够准确刻画疾病严重程度、具有强临床相关性、保留纵向疾病演变信息，并能预测未来的疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种定量、基于影像的表示方法，能够超越传统诊断标签和临床评分，实现对阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [94] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EventTime，一个多分辨率框架，用于预测网络安全事件披露后股票市场的短期异常损失。该框架结合长/短期市场历史上下文与事件元数据，并通过动态对比学习目标缓解稀疏监督问题，在构建的SECURE数据集上优于多种最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络攻击等外部事件通过离散记录披露，但其对金融市场的影响在连续时间序列中展开。现有时间序列预测模型专注于趋势、季节性、自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异构的外部事件（如网络安全披露）。同时，此类高影响事件稀疏且伴随市场噪声，进一步加剧了预测难度。

Method: EventTime采用多分辨率框架，融合长时程市场上下文、短时程事件前动态和事件元数据。核心包含：(1)事件融合模块，将时间表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关的近期市场模式；(2)动态对比学习目标，在训练中构造事件和时间序列感知的正负样本对，以缓解稀疏监督问题。同时构建SECURE真实数据集，将网络安全事件与股票市场时间序列及结构化/LLM派生语义特征对齐。

Result: 实验表明EventTime在估计事件后金融损失方面一致优于最先进的时序预测和事件感知基线。进一步分析显示其具有更事件敏感的表征、对不完整元数据更强的鲁棒性，以及对网络安全披露后短期市场影响更具可解释性的估计。

Conclusion: EventTime有效解决了事件条件影响预测问题，即从事件前的市场历史与有限的元数据估计披露后的短期异常损失（而非重建完整事件后轨迹），为安全事件与金融市场的交叉研究提供了新的方法框架和数据集。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [95] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DICS（数据信息质心分裂）框架，利用聚类和数据驱动先验构建紧凑的候选分裂集，在保持分类树预测性能的同时大幅降低训练时间，并可集成到决策树、随机森林和梯度提升模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树模型在机器学习中被广泛使用，但在大型高维数据集上训练成本高昂，主要瓶颈在于每个节点上对候选分裂的穷举搜索计算开销过大。

Method: 提出基于聚类的DICS框架，利用数据驱动的先验（包括类感知结构）构建紧凑且信息丰富的候选分裂集，大幅缩减分裂搜索空间；同时提供理论分析证明在给定假设下DICS不会降低分类树的性能。

Result: 在合成数据集和基准数据集上的大量实验表明，DICS在保持与穷举搜索相当的精度的同时，显著减少了训练时间，并适用于分类树、随机森林和梯度提升模型。

Conclusion: 将数据信息先验整合到分裂选择中，是实现可扩展分类树学习的有效途径，在效率和精度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [96] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，将表格数据中的正常样本转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，并通过提示词生成代码来构建评分引擎，实现无需微调的异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常检测具有挑战性，因为异常样本通常是跨特征依赖的违反而非简单边际偏差。现有方法（几何方法、重建方法）以及基于LLM的方法（微调或合成异常生成）都有局限，需要一种利用LLM上下文学习能力、无需微调的结构化异常检测方法。

Method: LLM-Detector将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖关系和蒸馏原型，组织成提示词用于生成代码。所产生的评分引擎从统计偏差、结构不一致性和密度异常三个维度计算每个测试样本的异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上与15个SOTA基线对比，在混合类型和纯连续型设置下均取得一致的性能提升，且无需LLM微调或神经网络训练，计算成本大幅降低。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector展示了通过结构化提示词引导LLM进行评分合成能够有效捕捉跨特征依赖中的异常模式，实现了高效、实用的真实世界表格系统异常检测。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [97] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过3,933次实验运行，在统一系统模型中显式建模重训练预算与训练加部署延迟，对比周期性重训练、误差阈值触发和ADWIN统计漂移触发三种策略与不重训练基线。核心发现是：最关键的决策不是重训练策略，而是部署模型是否支持增量学习。在线性增量学习器且标签即时可得的条件下，54组成对比较中没有任何策略与不重训练基线存在实际显著差异；而在无增量更新时，策略选择导致15-55个百分点的漂移后准确率差异，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于反应式策略，仅递归漂移下反应式策略有优势。论文还记录了反应式策略的系统性失效模式以及延迟-预算队列交互导致有效预算减半的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能会退化，但实践者缺乏关于何时重训练的严格指导。重训练成本高昂、预算有限，且训练和部署延迟意味着重训练后的模型不能立即生效，期间过期模型仍持续服务。因此需要一个受控的实证研究来严格比较不同模型刷新策略在明确的系统模型下的实际表现。

Method: 采用受控实证研究方法，在统一系统模型下显式建模重训练预算与训练加部署延迟。比较三种实用模型刷新策略（周期性重训练、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发）与不重训练基线。实验涵盖3,933次运行，跨越三种漂移机制、三个预算水平、最多五个延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式。并引入延迟-预算队列交互分析，揭示反应式策略的失效机制。

Result: 1) 当模型支持逐样本增量更新（在线线性学习器且即时标签）时，54组成对比较中没有策略与不重训练基线有实际显著差异，即使极端延迟下亦然。2) 无增量更新时，策略选择导致15-55个百分点漂移后准确率差异，周期性重训练在突变和渐变漂移下显著优于两种反应式策略，反应式策略仅在递归漂移下占优。3) 反应式策略存在系统性失效模式，且延迟-预算队列交互会静默地将有效重训练预算减半。

Conclusion: 模型刷新策略的选择重要性远低于部署模型是否支持增量学习这一架构决策。在支持增量更新的场景下，重训练策略几乎无关紧要；在无增量更新时，简单周期性重训练通常是比复杂的反应式漂移触发策略更稳妥的选择。论文同时指出反应式策略的失效模式与队列交互问题，并开源完整仿真器、数据管道和每次运行的产物以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作能力层级（DBOSC证书），用于检验多模态世界模型中不同传感器编码是否具有可互换的执行语义，以及这种语义能否在新型动作组合中存活。通过在触觉音频/加速度表示和受控弹塑性系统的实验，区分了属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行四个独立可测试的能力层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态世界模型将紧凑表示作为感知与物理交互之间的接口，但已有探针无法回答两个关键问题：不同传感器是否携带相同的可执行语义，以及这种语义在未见过的动作组合下是否仍然成立。需要一个可操作的检验框架来区分这些能力。

Method: 引入DBOSC（Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate）方法：让独立训练的多模态编译器在冻结的响应图上，针对训练集之外的证据进行互换性检验。设计了操作能力层级，并在Cluster Haptic触觉数据和受控弹塑性系统上进行了实验，包括预注册预算条件下的有序执行测试和融合闭包分析。

Result: 在Cluster Haptic上，同一未见表面的音频和加速度表征在响应空间中的距离比错误表面对近4.5倍，19个留出表面均保持该差距。在弹塑性系统中，预注册预算下执行器无法通过门槛，但在收敛预算下秩三图可以执行程序（oracle NMSE 0.18），融合优于单模态，16项检查中14项通过；两个失败源于融合信息矩阵的对角限制与完整矩阵性能相当。执行器而非图决定门槛通过与否，输出整体程序的执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍。

Conclusion: 属性访问、响应替换、融合闭包和有序执行是四个相互独立、可分别测试的能力成就。压缩和融合本身无法确定未见组合定律（存在非可识别性结果），门槛通过是执行器属性而非图属性，这为多模态世界模型的评估提供了更精细的框架。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart.
