Method: 提出DQeND（Deep Q-learning based Early and Non-stationary Decision）架构，基于强化学习框架，将表示学习、分类决策与触发决策纳入统一端到端优化目标，使其能与先进可分离基线保持直接可比性，并在受控漂移场景下进行系统性对比。

Result: 在多种漂移类型和强度场景下，DQeND表现出一致的鲁棒性，持续超越可分离基线。消融实验表明联合更新表示模块与决策模块是取得性能增益的关键因素，验证了端到端联合学习在动态环境中的优势。

Conclusion: 端到端学习能够为动态环境下的ECTS提供更好的自适应能力，研究结果支持探索替代可分离设计的新方向，为后续研究提供了激励和基准。

Abstract: Early Classification of Time Series (ECTS) requires making accurate decisions as early as possible in inherently online and evolving environments. Yet, most existing methods assume stationarity and rely on separable designs, where classification and triggering are optimized independently, an assumption that fundamentally limits their adaptability under drift. In this work, we challenge this paradigm and study ECTS under non-stationary conditions. We provide the first systematic comparison between separable and end-to-end approaches across controlled drifting scenarios. Building on Reinforcement Learning, we introduce DQeND, a unified architecture that jointly learns representation, classification, and triggering decisions, while remaining directly comparable to state-of-the-art separable baselines. Across a wide range of drifts, DQeND demonstrates strong robustness across various non-stationary scenarios, consistently outperforming separable baselines. An ablation study further highlights that jointly updating representation and decision modules is critical to these gains. Overall, our results indicate that end-to-end learning can offer improved adaptation capabilities for ECTS in dynamic environments, and motivate further investigation of alternatives to separable designs.

</details>


### [132] [DecoVAE: a Lightweight Interpretable Trend-Seasonal VAE Framework for Efficient Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20052)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DecoVAE 是一个轻量级、可解释的"趋势-季节"变分自编码器（VAE）框架，通过对时间序列进行显式分解，并使用差分正则化器约束趋势潜在轨迹的平滑性（类比Hodrick-Prescott滤波器），同时用复数高斯VAE在频域中捕捉季节分量的幅度和相位。在七个真实基准上，DecoVAE 在短期和长期预测中均优于强基线（CRPS最多降低14.96%，NMAE最多降低23.30%；长期分别达52.68%和26.51%），同时模型权重减少93%、速度提升74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率时间序列预测中，趋势和季节动态具有不同的内在属性，现有方法要么无法捕捉这些组件的独特特性，要么缺乏可解释性，要么存在严重的显存和运行开销问题。

Method: DecoVAE 框架将时间序列显式分解为趋势和季节两个流。趋势流利用差分正则化器强制潜在轨迹的结构性平滑（类似于Hodrick-Prescott滤波）；季节流在频域中通过复数高斯VAE操作，天然捕捉周期模式的幅度和相位。两个流分别施加领域特定的归纳偏置。

Result: 在七个真实世界基准测试中，DecoVAE 一致优于强基线。短期预测 CRPS 最多降低14.96%、NMAE 最多降低23.30%；长期预测分别最多降低52.68%和26.51%。同时模型权重比次优方法减少高达93%，速度提升高达74%。

Conclusion: 通过显式的趋势-季节分解和针对性的归纳偏置，DecoVAE 在显著提升概率预测精度的同时大幅压缩了模型体积与推理开销，实现了精度与效率的兼得。

Abstract: Probabilistic time series forecasting remains challenging, largely because modeling distinct trend and seasonal dynamics requires specialized approaches. Existing methods often fail to capture the unique inner properties of these components, lack interpretability, or suffer from heavy memory and runtime overhead. To address these limitations, we propose DecoVAE, a lightweight interpretable trend-seasonal VAE framework that explicitly decomposes time series into trend and seasonal components by applying domain-specific inductive biases. The trend stream enforces structural smoothness using a differential regularizer on the latent trajectory, analogous to the Hodrick-Prescott filter. Concurrently, the seasonal stream operates in the frequency domain via a complex Gaussian VAE, natively capturing the amplitude and phase of periodic patterns. Extensive evaluations across seven real-world benchmarks show that DecoVAE consistently outperforms strong baselines. It achieves reductions of up to 14.96\% in CRPS and 23.30\% in NMAE for short-term forecasting, and up to 52.68\% and 26.51\% for long-term horizons. Crucially, DecoVAE yields these accuracy gains while remaining highly efficient, reducing model weight by up to 93\% and accelerating speed by up to 74\% compared to the second-best method.

</details>


### [133] [Let's Scale Step by Step: Compute-Efficient Hyperparameter Transfer for Large-Scale Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20061)
*Nayeon Kim,Hojin Lee,Yunju Bak,Jaesun Park,Boseop Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种计算高效的两步超参数迁移框架，用于估计大规模 MoE 模型训练的最优学习率：先在模型宽度维度上通过 μP 适配进行迁移，再沿 token 维度通过预测性标度律外推到万亿 token 的训练规模。在 155B 总参数（17B 激活）的模型上验证了该方法，R²=0.95 的预测保真度表明小规模代理模型足以确定大规模训练的最优学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 架构可以显著扩充模型容量而不成比例增加计算成本，但在极端模型规模和 token 预算下，通过暴力搜索（sweeping）来优化超参数（尤其是学习率）在计算上是不可行的。因此需要一种计算高效的方法来预测大规模 MoE 训练的最优学习率。

Method: 提出两步骤框架：(1) 为采用多头潜在注意力（MLA）和 Muon 优化器的 MoE 架构设计最大更新参数化（μP）适配，证明最优学习率可跨宽度缩放模型一致迁移；(2) 沿 token 维度扩展可迁移性，建立预测性标度律，通过对小规模代理模型在有限预算下得到的最优值进行线性回归，外推至大规模训练视野（如 10 万亿 token）的学习率。

Result: 线性回归外推达到 R²=0.95 的高保真度；将该方法应用于从零预训练 155B 总参数（17B 激活参数）的基础模型，稳定训练和评测结果验证了全规模目标模型的最优配置可以被准确预测。

Conclusion: 小规模代理训练足以确定大规模 MoE 训练的最优学习率，以极低的消融成本即可精确预测全规模目标模型的最优配置，避免了大规模超参数扫描带来的计算开销。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures significantly expand model capacity without a proportional increase in computational cost. However, optimizing their hyperparameters---particularly the learning rate---at extreme scales of both model size and token budget via sweeping remains computationally prohibitive. In this paper, we propose a compute-efficient, two-step hyperparameter transfer framework that estimates optimal learning rates for training large MoE models by transferring them across scaling model widths, and subsequently extrapolating to trillion-token horizons. First, we formulate a Maximal Update Parameterization ($μ$P) adaptation for MoE architectures utilizing Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) and the Muon optimizer, demonstrating that optimal learning rates transfer consistently across width-scaled models. Second, we extend this transferability along the token dimension by establishing a predictive scaling law. By applying linear regression to the optimal values derived from small proxy models on limited budgets, we successfully extrapolate the ideal learning rate to massive training horizons (e.g., 10 trillion tokens) with high fidelity ($R^2=0.95$). Consequently, this indicates that proxy training on small models is sufficient to determine the optimal learning rate for the extensive training of large-scale MoEs. We apply the proposed methodology to pretrain our foundation model (155B total, 17B active parameters) from scratch, and the stable training and evaluation results validate that optimal configurations for full-scale target models can be accurately predicted with minimal ablation costs.

</details>


### [134] [Orthogonal JEPA: Factorized Predictive States for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20065)
*Taoyong Cui,Pheng Ann Heng,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于正交预测分解的潜在世界模型框架\method，将JEPA中单一的目标嵌入和预测通路分解为多个正交的预测分量，每个分量由独立的预测分支从共享上下文表示中估计，并通过正交性、因子活动性和方差正则化防止编码器塌缩，适用于多种预测目标和下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准JEPA将所有可预测内容通过单一目标嵌入和单一预测通路组织，在复杂系统中这种单块结构会将冗余容量分配给主导信号，而对次要预测结构产生弱梯度或冲突梯度，限制了潜在状态的质量。

Method: \method框架基于正交预测分解：学习基矩阵将目标状态分解为多个分量，每个分量由专用预测分支从共享上下文表示中估计。采用预测回归保持因子幅度以支持状态合成；正交性目标抑制重复方向；因子活动正则化维持投影目标的变化性；在线方差正则化防止坐标级编码器塌缩。预测分量合成完整潜在状态，可用于读出头、解码器、规划器或自回归 rollout。该机制适用于时间未来、空间隐藏或其他部分观测作为目标的情形。

Result: 在受控视觉、单细胞转录组学、纵向健康记录、连续控制和分子动力学等实验中，\method在表征质量、预测、规划和长时程稳定性方面均取得优于基线的结果。

Conclusion: 正交预测因子分解能够克服单块JEPA的容量分配问题，为复杂系统中学习通用、稳定的潜在世界状态提供了一种统一且有效的框架。

Abstract: World models construct latent states that support prediction, planning, and reasoning about an underlying system. Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) offer a direct way to learn such states by predicting targets in representation space instead of reconstructing every detail of the observation. Standard JEPAs, however, organize all predictable content through one target embedding and one prediction pathway. In complex systems, this monolithic state can allocate redundant capacity to dominant signals while providing weak or conflicting gradients to less dominant predictive structure. We introduce \method, a latent world-modeling framework based on orthogonal predictive factorization. Learned basis matrices analyze each target state into multiple components, and a dedicated prediction branch estimates each component from a shared context representation. Predictive regression preserves the factor magnitudes required for state synthesis, an orthogonality objective discourages repeated directions, factor-activity regularization maintains variation in projected targets, and online variance regularization discourages coordinate-wise encoder collapse. Predicted components are synthesized into a complete latent state that can be used by a readout, decoder, planner, or autoregressive rollout. The same predictive-state mechanism applies when the target is temporally future, spatially hidden, or another partial observation of the same system. Experiments on controlled vision, single-cell transcriptomics, longitudinal health records, continuous control, and molecular dynamics evaluate representation quality, forecasting, planning, and long-horizon stability.

</details>


### [135] [SAE-Xplainers: Rule-Based Feature Interpretation for Extreme Earth Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20117)
*Hugo Porta,Emanuele Dalsasso,Chang Xu,Theo Gnassounou,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于地理位置的稀疏自编码器（SAE）改进方法及其解释框架（SAE-Xplainers），用于提高极端地球事件（如火灾、热带气旋、大气河）深度学习预测模型的可解释性。通过地理位置调制SAE输入以捕捉环境模式的局部语义，并利用集成规则解释器将高维特征转换为符合科学文献的人类可理解规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天气与气候数据集为深度学习建模极端地球事件提供了新机遇，但在实际运营场景中，模型缺乏可解释性严重制约了其应用。虽然SAE在文本和图像模态中已被证明能提取人类可理解的概念，但由于天气与气候数据的特殊性质（空间异质性、多模态、复杂依赖），SAE直接用于极端事件分析仍面临挑战。

Method: 本文提出两个核心创新：（1）地理位置的输入调制（location-based input modulation）：通过对SAE输入进行基于地理位置的调制，使模型能够捕捉环境模式在特定区域的局部语义含义；（2）基于规则的SAE-Xplainers集成：利用规则集成解释器将SAE从复杂多模态环境预测变量中提取的高维特征展开为人类可理解的规则。

Result: 在三种极端事件类型（火灾预测、热带气旋检测、大气河检测）上的实验表明：（1）SAE输入调制显著提升了重构性能和特征利用率；（2）SAE-Xplainers能够将复杂的天气气候模式忠实地解释为与科学文献一致的人类可理解规则；（3）该方法还支持对特征吸收（feature absorption）现象的识别。

Conclusion: 本文证明通过地理位置感知的SAE调制与规则化解释器相结合，能够在保持深度学习模型性能的同时有效提升极端天气与气候事件建模的可解释性，为SAE从常规模态扩展到空间-时间异质性的环境科学领域提供了可行路径，并助力推动此类模型在实际运营场景中的采纳。

Abstract: The emergence of large-scale Weather and Climate (W&C) datasets offers new opportunities for modeling extreme Earth events (ExEE) and their impacts using deep learning. However, their adoption in operational settings remains limited by the lack of models' interpretability. While for conventional text and image modalities, tools such as Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have proven effective for extracting human-understandable concepts, their use for the analysis of ExEE remains challenging due to the nature of W&C data. To address this, we introduce (i) a geographic location-based modulation of the inputs of SAE to capture the local semantic meaning of environmental patterns, and (ii) an ensemble of rule-based SAE-Xplainers to interpret the resulting high-dimensional features derived from complex, multi-modal environmental predictors. We evaluate our method on three ExEE types: the prediction of fires, and the detection of tropical cyclones and atmospheric rivers. We show that SAE input modulation improves both reconstruction performance and feature utilization, and that our SAE-Xplainers enable faithful interpretation of complex climatic patterns by unfolding them into human-understandable rules that are consistent with the scientific literature, while also supporting the identification of feature absorption.

</details>


### [136] [Evaluating Neural Cartographic Relief Shading for Urban Environments: A Downtown Calgary Study Using High-Resolution DEM and DSM Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20149)
*Emmanuel Stefanakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在高分辨率DEM和DSM数据下，分析式与基于神经网络的明暗地形（hillshading）方法在密集城市环境（卡尔加里市中心）中的表现。研究发现，尽管Eduard（基于机器学习、以高山地形训练的系统）并非为城市建筑、桥梁、街道等设计，但通过参数调整仍能产生视觉上强烈且具有制图学价值的阴影效果，在某些方面优于传统分析式方法，同时也存在因训练偏差导致的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分析式明暗地形方法在城市环境中表现有限，而基于机器学习的Eduard系统虽针对自然山地景观训练，但其能否适应高密度建成环境尚待验证。研究旨在探索神经方法在城市地形表征中的适应性与局限性，填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: 采用探索性对比研究方法：（1）对比单方向和多方向分析式明暗地形与Eduard系统生成的浮雕阴影；（2）以卡尔加里市中心的高分辨率DEM/DSM为测试数据；（3）重点调节地形类型、宏观/微观泛化及平坦区域细节参数；（4）在整个神经实验中保持大规模着色风格恒定。

Result: 研究发现分析式明暗地形在某些场景下仍然更可靠；Eduard在特定城市环境中展现出意料之外的优势；但神经方法也因对高山地形的训练偏差而在部分城市要素上表现失败。参数调优确实能够产生视觉强烈且制图学上有用的结果，部分甚至优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 研究论证了针对自然地貌设计的神经地形渲染模型经参数调整后可部分适应城市建成环境，但通用性受限。作者主张未来应专门针对城市浮雕阴影进行模型训练与评估，而非简单复用面向自然地形的现有系统。

Abstract: This article explores the performance of analytical and neural-based hillshading methods in a dense urban environment using high-resolution digital elevation model (DEM) and digital surface model (DSM) data for downtown Calgary. The study compares single-direction and multi-direction analytical hillshading with relief shading generated in Eduard, a machine-learning system originally developed to emulate Swiss-style shaded relief trained primarily on mountainous landscapes. Because Eduard was not designed for buildings, bridges, streets, trees, and other urban infrastructures, the central question is not whether it perfectly reproduces urban morphology, but whether parameter tuning can nevertheless produce visually strong, cartographically useful, and in some cases superior results when compared with conventional analytical methods. The analysis focuses especially on terrain type, micro and macro generalization, and flat-area detail parameters, while keeping the large-scale shading style constant throughout the neural experiments. The article is structured as an exploratory comparison rather than a benchmark of universal best practice. It aims to identify where analytical hillshading remains more reliable, where Eduard offers unexpected strengths, and where neural shading fails because of its training bias toward alpine terrain. The study contributes to current work on terrain representation by testing whether a neural approach designed for natural landforms can be adapted to a highly built urban setting, and it concludes by arguing for future model training and evaluation specifically targeted at urban relief shading.

</details>


### [137] [Ask Self, Ask Others: Relation Is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20172)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Relation，一种替代Attention的token混合原语。它先将两两证据组织成显式的Self和Exchange关系，再推导信息流。实验表明Full Relation在10M、30M、100M三个尺度下均取得比MHA更低的验证NLL，FlashRelation比物化的Full Relation快3.60-4.41倍，并达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐的76.4-84.9%。该结果支持"关系优先"的token混合观点：先问Self，再问Others，然后让Flow跟随Relation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Attention直接从两两分数推导归一化信息流，本文质疑这一范式，主张先显式组织token间的语义关系（Self与Exchange），再基于关系推导信息流，从而为token混合提供更结构化的替代方案。

Method: 论文提出Relation作为一种新的token混合原语：首先将两两证据组织为显式的Self关系（token自身）与Exchange关系（token间），然后从这些关系推导信息流。在此基础上衍生出五种变体：Full Relation（完整关系）、FlashRelation（内存高效实现）、Linear Relation（线性复杂度）、Hybrid Relation（混合架构）以及KV风格的Relation Cache。模型为decoder-only架构，在约10M、30M和100M参数尺度下进行对比评估。

Result: Full Relation在三个参数尺度（10M/30M/100M）上的最终验证NLL均低于MHA基线。FlashRelation在固定上下文参考基准上比物化的Full Relation实现快3.60-4.41倍。在匹配规模的生产工作负载上，FlashRelation在执行完整Full Relation算子的同时达到PyTorch FlashAttention吞吐的76.4-84.9%。Hybrid Relation（75% Linear Relation层）在语言建模质量上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持一种"关系优先"（relation-first）的token混合观点：先询问Self，再询问Others，最后让Flow（信息流）跟随Relation。Relation原语及其多种变体在质量与效率上均具备与MHA竞争甚至超越的潜力。

Abstract: Attention directly derives normalized information flow from pairwise scores. We introduce Relation, an alternative token-mixing primitive that first organizes pairwise evidence into explicit Self and Exchange relations and derives information flow afterward. This relational organization gives rise to Full Relation, FlashRelation, Linear Relation, Hybrid Relation, and a KV-style Relation Cache. Across matched decoder-only models at approximately 10M, 30M, and 100M parameters, Full Relation achieves lower final validation NLL than MHA at all three scales. In a fixed-context reference benchmark, FlashRelation is 3.60-4.41x faster than the materialized Full Relation implementation. Across scale-matched production workloads, it reaches 76.4-84.9% of PyTorch FlashAttention throughput while executing the Full Relation operator. Hybrid Relation uses 75% Linear Relation layers and achieves strong language-modeling quality. These results support a relation-first view of token mixing: ask Self, ask Others, then let Flow follow Relation.

</details>


### [138] [A Standardized Framework for Machine Learning in Power System Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20181)
*Julian Oelhaf,Georg Kordowich,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro,Christian Bergler,Johann Jäger,Andreas Maier,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向标准化评估的框架，用于机器学习电力系统保护，定义七个必需的研究维度，并在PROTECT-90电磁暂态基准上进行实例化案例研究，验证MLP分类/定位性能及不同评估设置的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习电力系统保护研究中频繁报告接近完美的分数，但这些分数的意义强烈依赖于评估设置。保护任务、物理范围、测量、时序、目标、预处理和验证常常联合变化且说明不完整，导致结果难以比较和审计。

Method: 提出标准化导向框架，将评估设计视为科学贡献的一部分，定义七个必需研究维度：保护目标、物理范围、可观测性、时序与决策窗口、目标与样本有效性、验证协议、评估输出。在PROTECT-90基准的9022个模拟场景上实例化，进行故障分类与定位研究。

Result: 集中感知、20ms窗口、按场景分组验证下，MLP分类宏平均F1为0.991±0.001，定位MAE为线路长度的10.20±0.25%。扩展到50ms决策时窗保持任务依赖的性能不对称性；降低可观测性使定位误差约翻倍而对分类影响甚微；传统双端定位器在更丰富信息下优于学习型定位器。

Conclusion: 框架将评估假设转化为明确、可复现的证据，为机器学习保护功能提供更具可比性、可审计的评估基础，并为未来面向认证的评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Studies of machine-learning-based power-system protection increasingly report near-perfect scores, yet the meaning of those scores depends strongly on the evaluation setting. Protection task, physical scope, measurements, timing, targets, preprocessing, and validation often vary jointly and remain incompletely specified. This paper proposes a standardization-oriented framework that treats evaluation design as part of the scientific contribution. It defines seven required study dimensions: protection objective, physical scope, observability, timing and decision windows, targets and sample validity, validation protocol, and evaluation outputs. The framework is instantiated in a bounded case study on the public PROTECT-90 electromagnetic-transient benchmark, comprising 9022 simulated episodes from a 90 kV double-line topology, for onset-conditioned fault classification and localization. Under centralized sensing, simulation-metadata-aligned 20 ms windows, and episode-grouped validation, a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) achieved a five-fold mean macro-averaged F1 score of 0.991 +/- 0.001 for classification and a localization mean absolute error of 10.20 +/- 0.25% of line length (mean +/- std across episode-grouped folds). Extending the decision horizon to 50 ms preserved this task-dependent performance asymmetry, while reduced observability approximately doubled the MLP localization error but had little effect on classification. A synchronized two-ended conventional locator outperformed the learning locators under its richer clean information set, and measurement degradation showed that clean predictive performance did not determine robustness. The framework turns evaluation assumptions into explicit, reproducible evidence and provides a basis for more comparable, auditable evaluation and future certification-oriented assessment of machine-learning protection functions.

</details>


### [139] [Decoding silent reading from non-invasive EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20186)
*Ingo Marquardt,Anthilia Alchanat,Priyanka Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将无声阅读作为解码内心言语的高效代理任务，利用约240,000个单词展示的19通道干电极EEG数据，通过CLIP式对比学习架构（卷积编码器+可选的因果Transformer），成功实现了开放词表级别的词汇与语义信息解码。结果显示解码性能可靠高于随机基线，可推广至中频和稀有词，且随训练数据量呈对数线性增长而无饱和迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接收集伴随自发内心独白的脑活动-文本配对数据在根本上是不可行的，而现有的替代范式（线索化重复性内心言语和回顾性报告生成性内心言语）获取速度慢、时间对齐差且难以验证被试依从性。因此需要寻找一种可扩展的代理任务来研究内心言语解码。

Method: 以快速序列视觉呈现（RSVP）方式呈现连续叙述文本中的单词，并在每次试验中随机化字体以部分去相关单词身份与低级视觉形式。采用卷积EEG编码器（可选地接上因果Transformer），通过CLIP式对比学习目标将短EEG窗口与大型语言模型的隐藏层嵌入对齐。评估采用词分组的top-10检索对置换基线。控制分析分离词级解码、叙事上下文追踪以及Transformer位置编码引入的非神经位置先验。

Result: 解码性能可靠高于机会水平，能扩展到中频和稀有词，随训练数据量呈对数线性增长且无饱和迹象。去除枕叶和后颞电极会使词级增益降低约三分之一，但上下文追踪不变。

Conclusion: 实验证明开放词表的词级信息可以从无声阅读时的EEG中恢复，且当前解码是数据受限的而非饱和的，暗示更多数据可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Non-invasive decoding of inner speech faces a fundamental data problem: a corpus pairing brain activity with a person's spontaneous inner monologue cannot be collected, and the available proxy paradigms (cued repetitive and retrospectively reported generative inner speech) are slow to acquire, poorly time-locked, and subject compliance is unverifiable. We therefore treat silent reading as a scalable proxy task and ask how much lexical and semantic information a contrastive decoder can extract from it. We report an open-vocabulary analysis of approximately 240,000 word presentations recorded from a single densely-sampled participant across 393 runs (ca. 49 h) of 19-channel dry-electrode EEG. Words from continuous narrative text were presented in rapid serial visual presentation, with typography randomised on every trial to partially decorrelate word identity from low-level visual form. A convolutional EEG encoder, optionally followed by a causal transformer, was trained with a CLIP-style contrastive objective to align short EEG windows with hidden-state embeddings of the presented word taken from a large language model. Decoding, evaluated as word-grouped top-10 retrieval against permutation baselines, was reliably above chance, extended to mid-frequency and rare words, and scaled log-linearly with training-data volume with no sign of saturation. Removing occipital and posterior-temporal electrodes reduced the word-level gain by roughly one third but left context tracking unchanged. Control analyses separate word-level decoding from narrative context tracking and from a non-neural positional prior introduced by the transformer's positional embedding. These results establish that open-vocabulary word-level information is recoverable from EEG during silent reading, and that decoding is data-limited rather than saturated.

</details>


### [140] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文使用PCMCI+算法从家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试（HSAT）记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，并通过边缘黑名单和引导聚合等方法，揭示了不同性别和年龄亚队列之间因果结构的系统差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动态复杂且在不同患者群体中差异显著，阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发。作者希望通过学习不同亚队列的动态因果结构，识别群体间的系统差异，为精准干预提供依据。

Method: 采用PCMCI+算法对105个HSAT记录中的窗口化分数变量进行动态因果图学习，利用领域知识进行边缘黑名单限制，并通过引导聚合（bootstrap aggregation）处理小亚队列样本量不足的问题。

Result: 学习到的因果图显示，时间自依赖性和呼吸暂停-脱氧饱和度之间的关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他因果关系在不同性别和年龄亚队列间表现出显著差异。

Conclusion: 动态因果图学习能够揭示睡眠呼吸障碍在不同患者亚群间的因果结构差异，其中某些核心因果关系具有跨群体普遍性，而其他关系则因人群而异，这为面向特定人群的精准干预开发提供了重要线索。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [141] [Physical-Support Confidence Sets for Highly Coherent Dictionaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20295)
*Guan-Ju Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分辨率感知的物理支撑推断方法，该方法联合考虑学习字典的不确定性和部署信号表示的稀疏性，通过跨字典置信对应关系保留与校准数据和部署数据兼容的解释，并投影到物理支撑空间。理论结果表明最小最大物理分辨率 δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s, 1/(√N s²)}，并设计了活动端点括号化算法（AEB）以避免过度精确的物理推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高相干字典中，字典学习后的稀疏追求即使能得到精确的原子支撑，其物理解释也可能不被校准数据所合理支撑。不同但校准兼容的替代字典可能将相同支撑赋予不同的物理含义，因此需要一种能联合考虑字典不确定性与部署信号表示不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出跨字典置信对应方法，同时保留校准兼容的字典与部署兼容的稀疏表示，再将幸存解释投影到物理支撑空间。对于局部相干原子类（分离尺度为s），推导了N个校准信号下的最小最大物理分辨率。计算上引入活动端点括号化（AEB）自适应有限库过程，仅评估能影响物理报告的候选，否则安全地粗化或弃权。

Result: 理论结果表明最小最大物理分辨率满足 δ_opt(N,s)≍min{s, 1/(√N s²)}，相对分辨率受方向信息尺度 Ns⁶ 支配。部署复制仅在方向变化不能被活动系数调整吸收时才能改善物理定位。有限库实验（包括四区域合成应用）表明点值插件选择器可能物理上过度精确，而AEB以更少的候选评估避免了不支持的细化。

Conclusion: AEB方法能有效避免物理上过度精确的推断，以更高效的计算代价提供分辨率感知的物理支撑推断，为高相干字典场景下稀疏表示与物理可解释性之间的张力提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Sparse pursuit after dictionary learning can yield a precise atom support even when its physical interpretation is not justified by the calibration data, especially for highly coherent dictionaries where alternative calibration-compatible dictionaries may assign different physical meanings to the same selected support. We develop resolution-aware physical-support inference that jointly accounts for uncertainty in the learned dictionary and in the representation of a deployment signal. Our cross-dictionary confidence correspondence retains calibration-compatible dictionaries and deployment-compatible sparse representations, then projects the surviving explanations onto physical-support space. For local coherent-atom classes with separation scale s, once the deployment data resolve the coherent-block explanation and its atom support, the minimax physical resolution from N calibration signals satisfies $δ_{\mathrm{opt}}(N,s)\asymp\min\{s,\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}s^2}\}$, with relative resolution governed by the orientation-information scale $Ns^6$. Deployment replication improves physical localization only when orientation changes cannot be absorbed by adjusting the active coefficients. For computation, we introduce active endpoint bracketing (AEB), an adaptive finite-bank procedure that evaluates only candidates that can still affect the physical report and otherwise safely coarsens or abstains. Finite-bank experiments, including a four-region synthetic application, show that a point-valued plug-in selector can be physically overprecise, whereas AEB avoids unsupported refinement with fewer candidate evaluations.

</details>


### [142] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BERT-LER，一个针对结构化电子健康记录(EHR)的BERT风格模型，在7500万患者数据上预训练和微调，通过百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token以保留分级信息，并利用Integrated Gradients提供token级归因解释。在EHRShot基准和哮喘进展任务上，预测性能与公开基准相当或在实验室相关任务上更优，且归因与临床已知风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息和基于输入医疗事件的可解释性。作者旨在弥合这一方法论空白，在统一的框架中同时实现实验室值表征和可解释性，并评估预测性能与解释是否能在标准临床预测任务之外泛化。

Method: 采用BERT风格架构，在7500万患者的去标识化EHR时间线上预训练和微调。实验室检验结果通过百分位分箱编码为离散token，在保留分级信息的同时适配token化表示。解释方面使用Integrated Gradients生成基于输入EHR序列的token级归因。在EHRShot基准套件和基于真实世界数据的哮喘严重程度进展研究上进行基准测试。

Result: 在EHRShot和哮喘任务上，BERT-LER的预测性能与公开基准模型相当，且在实验室相关任务上通常超越基准；其提供的归因与临床上已知的风险因素保持一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构与可解释性方法可广泛应用于多种治疗领域和预测任务，适用于在结构化EHR上训练的语言模型，为EHR基础模型在实验室值表征与可解释性的统一上提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>


### [143] [A comparison between ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB and Wi-Fi radar for in-bedroom human activity monitoring and sleep interruption detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20322)
*Anton Lambrecht,Reda El Hail,Xianjun Jiao,Pieter Crombez,Dominique Schreurs,Peter Karsmakers,Adnan Shahid,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在相同部署条件下（20名参与者、6种房间布局、同一CNN架构）对天花板安装的FMCW雷达、IR-UWB和Wi-Fi感知三种射频技术进行受控对比，发现IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别上最优（89.0%宏观F1），FMCW在跨房间布局泛化上最佳（83.8%宏观F1），所有技术在睡眠监测中均超过92%宏观F1，并揭示了识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在评估不同射频技术（FMCW、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi）时，通常使用不同的硬件、数据集和评估方法，缺乏在相同条件下进行公平对比的研究。此外，天花板安装雷达在实际医疗保健环境中具有部署和成本优势，但其性能尚未得到充分探索。因此需要一项受控对比研究来提供公平且实用的技术评估。

Method: 采用20名参与者在6种房间布局下的同步录音数据，将三种天花板安装的射频技术（FMCW雷达、IR-UWB、Wi-Fi）置于完全相同的评估条件下，使用相同的卷积神经网络（CNN）架构，分别在细粒度10类人体活动识别（HAR）任务和粗粒度4类睡眠监测任务上进行评估。

Result: IR-UWB在跨受试者活动识别中达到最高性能（89.0%宏观F1）；FMCW在跨房间布局泛化上表现最佳（83.8%宏观F1）；所有技术在睡眠监测中在未见环境中均超过92%宏观F1。识别性能与环境鲁棒性之间存在根本性权衡，该权衡可通过距离分辨率、天线分集、多普勒分辨率和空间信息保留的差异来解释。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了射频技术识别精度与环境鲁棒性之间的权衡关系，为面向医疗保健的射频感知系统设计提供了实用指南。不同技术各有优势：IR-UWB适合追求高识别精度的场景，FMCW适合需要跨环境泛化的应用，而所有技术均能满足睡眠监测的需求。

Abstract: Despite their growing importance for contact-free radio frequency (RF) based healthcare monitoring, different radio technologies such as frequency-modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar, impulse radio ultra-wideband (IR-UWB), and Wi-Fi sensing are rarely compared under identical deployment conditions, as existing studies typically differ in hardware, datasets, and evaluation methodologies. In addition, the performance of ceiling-mounted radars, despite their practical deployment and cost advantages in healthcare environments, remain underexplored. Therefore, this paper presents a controlled comparison and analysis of ceiling-mounted FMCW, IR-UWB, and Wi-Fi sensing using synchronized recordings from 20 participants across six room layouts. All technologies are evaluated with the same convolutional neural network (CNN) on both a fine-grained 10-class human activity recognition (HAR) task and a coarse 4-class sleep monitoring task. IR-UWB achieves the highest cross-subject activity recognition performance (89.0% macro F1), while FMCW generalizes best to unseen room layouts (83.8% macro F1). For sleep monitoring, all technologies exceed 92% macro F1 in unseen environments. The results reveal a fundamental trade-off between recognition performance and environmental robustness, which can be explained through differences in range resolution, antenna diversity, Doppler resolution, and spatial information retention. These findings provide practical guidelines for the design of healthcare-oriented RF sensing systems.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Beyond linear subspaces: Nonlinear moment matching meets quadratic manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19486)
*Reetish Padhi,Serkan Gugercin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二次流形（quadratic manifold）的无优化、系统无关的模型降阶框架，可针对具有慢Kolmogorov n-width衰减的线性控制系统直接构造二次投影矩阵，并通过矩匹配和中心流形保持实现高保真降阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性子空间在慢Kolmogorov n-width衰减的线性控制系统中存在局限性，而基于二次流形的模型降阶虽有潜力，但现有的系统理论框架中尚缺乏构造此类二次近似的系统性方法。

Method: 提出一个系统无关（system-agnostic）、免优化（optimization-free）的框架，直接构造二次投影矩阵，无需求解优化问题；通过理论证明所合成的降阶模型能够匹配全阶系统的非线性矩，并精确保持中心流形映射。

Result: 在传输主导（transport-dominated）基准问题（一维阻尼波动方程和对流方程）上的数值实验表明，该方法在显著降低的降维状态空间中实现了高保真轨迹重构，带来可观的计算开销节省。

Conclusion: 该框架为慢Kolmogorov n-width问题提供了一种无需优化的直接二次降阶方案，在特定输入类下能确保稳态输出的渐近跟踪，兼具理论保障与计算效率。

Abstract: Quadratic manifold-based model order reduction offers a viable pathway to circumvent the limitations of linear subspaces for linear control systems characterized by slow Kolmogorov $n$-width decay. However, a system-theoretic framework for constructing such quadratic approximations remains absent from the literature. This paper presents a system-agnostic, optimization-free framework for the direct construction of quadratic projection matrices. We prove that the synthesized reduced-order model matches the nonlinear moments of the full-order system and preserves its exact center manifold mapping, thereby ensuring asymptotic tracking of steady-state outputs under specific input classes. Numerical results on transport-dominated benchmark problems, namely, the one-dimensional damped wave and advection equations, show that the proposed framework achieves high-fidelity trajectory reconstruction within a significantly reduced-dimensional state space, yielding substantial online computational savings.

</details>


### [145] [On the Value Function of Infinite-Horizon Optimal Control of Piecewise Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19530)
*Francesco Cordiano,Kanghui He,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分段仿射（PWA）系统中约束无限时域最优控制（CITOC）问题值函数的结构，发现值函数的分段仿射片段数目在紧致集上可能为无穷，并通过具体例子证明了这一可能性，同时给出了确保值函数为"真"PWA函数（即有限个仿射片段）的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（如[1]）已证明CITOC问题中PWA系统的值函数在状态空间中是分段仿射的，但没有分析该值函数是否为"真"PWA函数，即紧致集上是否具有有限个仿射片段。这一问题的回答对于值函数的结构理解及学习型控制方案的理论支撑具有重要意义。

Method: 通过构造一个显式反例来证明值函数的PWA片段数可以为无穷，并基于该例子进一步推导出易于验证的充分条件，确保值函数在紧致集上具有有限个仿射片段（即真PWA函数）。

Result: 证明了CITOC问题中PWA系统的值函数确实可能出现无穷个仿射片段的情况；同时给出了可验证的充分条件来保证值函数是真PWA函数，并通过数值示例对理论结果进行了说明。

Conclusion: 该工作补充了现有文献中关于PWA系统CITOC问题值函数结构的结果（如线性二次情形），为近期基于学习的PWA系统控制方案提供了理论支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of the value function in constrained infinite-time optimal control (CITOC) problems of piecewise affine (PWA) systems, with $\ell_1$ or $\ell_\infty$ stage cost. Existing works, such as [1], establish that the resulting value function is PWA in the state. However, existing results do not analyze whether the value function is a proper PWA function, i.e., with a finite number of affine pieces over compact sets, or whether the number of pieces can be infinite. We show that the latter case is indeed possible by means of an explicit example, which is also instrumental in establishing rigorous and easily verifiable sufficient conditions that ensure that the resulting value function is a proper PWA function. Our theoretical findings complement well-known results, e.g., the linear-quadratic case, and serve as support for recent learning-based control schemes for PWA systems. Throughout the paper, the proposed results are illustrated by means of a numerical example.

</details>


### [146] [Adaptive Time Windows for Discrete Adjoint Topology Optimization of Unsteady Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19617)
*Zongyuan Liu,Kentaro Yaji,Musaddiq Al Ali,Shengfeng Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于时间窗口序列的演化非定常流动目标评估与伴随优化框架，通过连续窗口收敛准则自动识别代表性时间窗口，并基于LBM-LES求解器实现湍流气动形状优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非定常流动优化需要先验地预设评估时间区间，这可能导致评估结果对区间选择敏感，缺乏客观性和一致性。为此需要一种能自动识别充分发展阶段代表性时间窗口的框架，以实现客观的目标评估和离散伴随分析。

Method: 框架核心包括：(1) 用时间窗口序列刻画每个演化中的非定常流场；(2) 引入连续窗口收敛准则，自动识别处于充分发展阶段的时间窗口；(3) 在识别的代表性时间窗口上一致地进行目标函数评估与离散伴随分析；(4) 采用基于正则化格子Boltzmann方法的大涡模拟（LBM-LES）求解器，配合部分反弹流固模型实现。

Result: 通过后向台阶流动验证了连续窗口收敛准则的有效性；圆柱绕流应用考察了不同流动状态对框架的影响；尾流流动恢复问题验证了其在非定常拓扑优化中的有效性；U型弯管优化进一步展示了框架识别和重组复杂涡结构的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自动识别代表性时间窗口，消除了对预先指定评估区间的依赖，为演化非定常流动的客观评估与优化提供了一致且有效的途径，并在多种流动状态和优化问题中得到了验证。

Abstract: Rather than prescribing an evaluation interval a priori, the proposed framework characterizes each evolving unsteady flow using a sequence of time windows. A consecutive-window convergence criterion is introduced to automatically identify a representative time window within its fully developed stage. The objective evaluation and discrete adjoint analysis are then carried out consistently over the identified representative time window. The framework is implemented using a regularized lattice Boltzmann method-based large-eddy simulation (LBM-LES) solver together with a partial bounce-back fluid-solid model. The consecutive-window convergence criterion is first validated using the backward-facing step flow. Cylinder-flow applications are then employed to investigate the influence of different flow regimes on the proposed framework. The wake-flow recovery problem verifies its effectiveness for unsteady topology optimization, while U-bend optimization further demonstrates its capability to identify and reorganize complex vortical structures.

</details>


### [147] [High-Multiplicity Flexible Job Shops: From Exact Recurrent Fluid Attainment to Structure-Guided Finite-Horizon Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19711)
*Wenjun Zheng,Wei Qu,Weilin Cai,Jianfeng Mao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究高多样性柔性作业车间调度问题，证明了最优流体分配经过有限缩放后可被可行重复离散调度精确实现，并基于此提出了类型基循环模板重放（TCTR）算法。在676个公共实例上，TCTR全部可行，平均间隙1.54%，优于作业索引自适应大邻域搜索（5.32%）和混合遗传算法（6.18%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性作业车间调度问题中，流体松弛提供了可扩展的工作量下界，但其分数机分配无法直接定义单个作业的可行调度。需要弥合流体松弛与可行离散调度之间的鸿沟，并设计无需随作业副本数增长的高效算法。

Method: 将流体分配进行有限缩放为整数操作数，在非重叠机器区间内放置操作，重复排列该结构，并在不移动任何区间的前提下跨重复连接操作形成单个作业，从而保持作业前序和机器可行性。基于该重复结构，提出类型基循环模板重放（TCTR），用规模不随作业副本数增长的优化模型搜索作业类型模板，然后在整个实例上重放所选模板。

Result: 在676个多样性扩展的公共柔性作业车间实例上，TCTR在所有情况下均可行，对流体下界的平均间隙为1.54%，优于作业索引自适应大邻域搜索（5.32%）和混合遗传算法（6.18%）。对于具有相同作业类型构成且含固定数量额外作业的增长有限实例，最优完成时间与流体下界之间的间隙保持有界常数，相对间隙随实例增长趋近于零。

Conclusion: 理论分析证明流体松弛的可行离散实现是可能的，且间隙有界；实际算法TCTR在保持模板规模独立于作业副本数的同时，显著优于现有方法，展示了类型基循环结构在解决高多样性柔性作业车间问题中的有效性和扩展性。

Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. We show that, after a suitable finite scaling, an optimal fluid allocation can be realized exactly by a feasible repeating discrete schedule. The construction scales the fluid allocation to integer operation counts, places the resulting operations in nonoverlapping machine intervals, repeats this arrangement, and links operations across repetitions into individual jobs without moving any interval, thereby enforcing job precedence while preserving machine feasibility. For growing finite instances with the same job-type composition, even with a fixed number of extra jobs, the gap between the optimal makespan and the fluid lower bound remains bounded by a constant; hence the relative gap vanishes as the instance grows. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. On 676 multiplicity-expanded public flexible-job-shop instances, TCTR is feasible in every case and achieves a 1.54% mean gap to the fluid lower bound, compared with 5.32% for a job-indexed adaptive large-neighborhood search and 6.18% for a hybrid genetic algorithm.

</details>


### [148] [Dangling points in area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19747)
*Beniamin Bogosel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Meissner多面体（由单位直径极值有限集生成的常宽体）中悬挂点对表面积最小化的影响。证明了删除悬挂点不会使最小表面积增大，添加悬挂点不会使最小表面积减小，从而将表面积最小化搜索范围缩减到不含悬挂点的生成集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meissner多面体是常宽体的一个重要类别，由极值有限点集生成。悬挂点是生成集中恰好有两个对径邻点的点，其能否在表面积最小化中发挥重要作用是一个未解决的问题。厘清这一问题有助于缩小寻找面积最小化Meissner多面体的搜索空间。

Method: 针对给定的极值集合，通过理论分析证明两个核心命题：(1) 删除一个悬挂点不会使基于该集合的Meissner多面体的最小表面积增加；(2) 添加一个悬挂点不会使最小可达到的表面积减小。这两个结论共同表明悬挂点在面积最小化中不起到不可替代的作用。

Result: 证明了删除悬挂点不会增大Meissner多面体的最小表面积，添加悬挂点不会减小最小可达到的表面积。因此，寻找面积最小化的Meissner多面体可以限定于不含悬挂点的极值生成集。

Conclusion: 悬挂点在Meissner多面体表面积最小化问题中并非本质因素，面积最小化搜索可安全地缩减至无悬挂点的生成集，这显著简化了问题的搜索空间。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. Such a generating set may contain dangling points, namely points having exactly two diametric neighbors. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. Adding a dangling point cannot decrease the smallest achievable surface area. This reduces the search for area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra to generating sets without dangling points.

</details>


### [149] [A Controllability Gramain Shaping with LMI Constraints under Bures--Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19754)
*Koju Nishimoto,Yuki Onishi,Riku Funada,Mitsuji Sampei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过塑造可控性格拉姆矩阵（controllability Gramian）为目标形式来设计外部输入对系统状态影响的控制器设计方法。利用Bures-Wasserstein距离将问题形式化为最小化系统格拉姆矩阵与期望格拉姆矩阵之间的距离，并证明目标函数在对称正定矩阵集合上严格凸。通过LMI推导出半定规划形式化描述，提升计算效率并可融入额外LMI约束。当外部输入为高斯白噪声时，该框架与H2控制密切相关，可视为最优传输的特例。数值实验在制导机器人上验证了各向异性可控性设计和额外方向性约束的能力，并确认当期望格拉姆矩阵趋于零时方法趋近H2控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制器设计方法难以灵活地设计外部输入到系统状态的映射效果，特别是实现方向性（各向异性）的可控性塑形。需要一种能够将可控性格拉姆矩阵塑造成任意期望形式的系统化设计框架。

Method: 基于Bures-Wasserstein距离对系统格拉姆矩阵与期望格拉姆矩阵之间的距离进行最小化，证明目标函数在SPD矩阵集合上严格凸；通过LMI推导SDP形式化描述，提升计算效率并支持额外约束；将H2控制解释为最优传输的一个特例。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法能够在制导机器人上实现各向异性可控性设计，并通过LMI施加额外方向约束；当期望格拉姆矩阵趋近于零时，所提方法收敛于H2控制。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种统一且计算高效的可控性格拉姆矩阵塑形框架，兼具凸优化的可解性和LMI的可扩展性，且与H2控制建立了理论联系，为最优控制提供新视角。

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. Using the Bures--Wasserstein distance, we formulate the shaping problem as the minimization of the distance between the system Gramian and a desired Gramian, and the objective function is shown to be strictly convex on the set of symmetric positive definite matrices. In addition, by deriving a semidefinite programming formulation via a linear matrix inequality (LMI), computational efficiency is improved and additional LMI constraints can be incorporated. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

</details>


### [150] [A Fixed-Penalty Linearized Augmented Lagrangian Method with Classical Multiplier Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19847)
*Benqi Liu,Kangkang Deng,Zichen Wang,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出非线性残差线性化增广拉格朗日方法（NR-LALM），用于光滑非凸确定性或随机目标下的等式约束优化。该方法用正则化高斯-牛顿型步骤替代昂贵的非线性原问题子问题，同时保留基于非线性约束残差的经典乘子更新。确定性情形下O(ε⁻²)迭代内找到ε近似KKT点；随机情形下期望O(ε⁻³)随机梯度评估和O(ε⁻²)约束/雅可比评估。所有理论结果在Lean 4中正式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法对非线性等式约束优化有效，但求解其非线性原问题子问题代价高昂。为降低每个外迭代的计算成本，需要一种既保留乘子更新结构、又避免完全求解非线性子问题的方法，同时保证收敛速率和复杂度不劣化。

Method: NR-LALM用正则化高斯-牛顿型步骤（由单个对称正定线性系统计算）替代非线性原问题子问题，同时保留基于非线性约束残差的经典乘子更新。分析中控制由于线性化原模型与非线性残差更新不匹配产生的二次约束线性化误差。对随机目标，采用带安全重启的投影随机路径积分微分估计器。可选的最小范数二阶修正将约束线性化误差从二阶降至四阶而复杂度阶数不变。所有结果在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Result: 确定性NR-LALM在固定、精度无关参数下，O(ε⁻²)次迭代及一阶预言机评估内找到ε近似KKT对。随机情形下期望O(ε⁻³)随机梯度评估、O(ε⁻²)约束及雅可比评估。紧性和Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz条件进一步保证确定性原始-对偶序列的有限长度收敛。数值实验验证了预测的误差阶数，在高维确定性和随机问题上表现良好。

Conclusion: NR-LALM通过线性化替代非线性子问题的方式显著降低增广拉格朗日方法的计算开销，同时保持理论收敛保证。乘子有界性与轨迹局部化由推导获得而非假设。经验证的理论结果与数值实验表明，该方法在确定性和随机非凸问题上均具有竞争力。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>


### [151] [To Control or not to Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19862)
*Odysseas Kanavetas,Camiel M. P. Koopmans,Floske M. Spieksma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带有控制休假（control vacations）的M/M/1队列模型，该模型在控制周期（使用较高服务速率）与固定服务速率周期之间切换。利用Markov决策过程框架，作者给出了平均期望成本能够降低的充分条件，并构造了成本降低策略。通过更新奖励过程与Tauberian定理建立了平均成本与消失折扣法的联系，解析地给出了平均成本降低的下界，并通过仿真验证了实际成本降低效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的队列控制模型通常假设控制机制永久生效，这在现实场景中不切实际——控制本身需要代价，且控制期间结束后系统可能需要恢复期（休假）。作者希望引入控制休假机制，使得控制仅在临时周期内施加，其余时间使用固定服务速率，从而在保证性能的同时降低总控制成本。

Method: 采用两速率M/M/1队列模型：控制周期长度指数分布后进入控制休假，休假期间使用固定服务速率μ，下次控制周期需在特定成本下调度。利用Kanavetas等人的马尔可夫决策过程框架推导成本降低的充分条件，构造成本降低策略；在特定策略下过程构成更新奖励过程，利用Tauberian定理将平均期望成本与消失折扣法关联，解析求平均成本降低下界，最终通过仿真验证。

Result: 推导出保证相对于仅使用固定服务速率模型能降低平均期望成本的充分条件；在此条件下构造了成本降低策略；解析确定了平均成本降低的下界；仿真研究展示了这些策略实际获得的成本降低幅度。

Conclusion: 控制休假机制可以在适当的条件下显著降低M/M/1队列的平均期望控制成本，且所提出的策略具有可解析验证的成本降低下界，仿真结果支持了理论分析的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

</details>


### [152] [Efficient flatness-based computation of trajectories for vehicles with many trailers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19915)
*Basile Graf,Gustave Lapierre,Philippe Müllhaupt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微分平坦性的高效算法，用于计算带n节拖挂车的汽车的前馈转向输入。算法结合四种关键技术（角中间变量、子映射因子化递归、缩放高阶乘积/商规则、基于截断形式幂级数的复合规则），将计算复杂度降至O(r³)，在r=40阶导数内耗时不足1毫秒，远优于SymPy和Bell多项式等参考方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带n节拖挂车的汽车的前馈转向输入计算可通过微分平坦性实现，但标准方法（对平坦输出的迭代符号微分）随拖挂车数量n呈超指数级增长，在n约等于5时即变得不可处理，亟需更高效的算法。

Method: 提出一种组合算法，包含四个组成部分：(1) 引入角中间变量简化表达式；(2) 将递归因子化为子映射以降低复杂度；(3) 使用缩放的更高阶乘积/商规则；(4) 采用基于截断形式幂级数的复合规则替代Faa di Bruno公式。

Result: 基准测试显示：SymPy在导数阶r=14时达到20秒超时，Bell多项式递归在r=24时超过60秒超时；而所提方法具有O(r³)算术复杂度，在r=40时仍保持1毫秒以内，且在参考方法可行的区域内与机器精度数值一致。作为端到端示例，成功计算并动画展示了20节拖挂车的停车操作。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效解决了带n节拖挂车汽车的微分平坦性计算中表达式爆炸的瓶颈问题，大大扩展了可处理问题的规模（从约5节拖挂车扩展到20节），且保持了数值精度，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: We address the practical computation of feedforward steering inputs for a car with n trailers via differential flatness. The standard route (iterated symbolic differentiation of the flat output) scales prohibitively with n: even with computer-algebra software the expression sizes grow super-exponentially and become intractable beyond n approx. 5. We propose an algorithm that combines four ingredients (angular intermediate variables, factorisation of the recursion in submaps, rescaled higher-order product/quotient rules, and a composition rule based on truncated formal power series instead of Faa di Bruno's formula). We benchmark the proposed method against (i) direct symbolic differentiation in SymPy and (ii) Faa di Bruno's formula evaluated via Bell polynomials. Both reference methods hit a clear computational wall: SymPy reaches a 20 s timeout at derivative order r = 14, while the Bell-polynomial recursion exceeds a 60 s timeout beyond r = 24. The proposed approach has O(r3) arithmetic complexity in the derivative order and remains under one millisecond up to r = 40, with numerical agreement to machine precision in the regime where the reference methods succeed. As an end-to-end illustration, a 20-trailer parking manoeuvre is computed and animated.

</details>


### [153] [Dual Control: On Exploration-Exploitation in Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20073)
*Tomas J. Meijer,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了线性时不变系统中"双控制"（同时对参数未知进行主动探测以达到探索与利用平衡）的理论与方法，涵盖四大研究方向：多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、最小遗憾率控制器和极小极大最优双控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双控制问题已被研究近一个世纪，但各研究方向分散在不同文献中，缺乏系统性梳理。特别是极小极大最优双控制器作为一个有前景的新框架，需要在更广泛的背景下予以定位和理解。

Method: 这是一篇综述性论文，系统回顾了四大研究方向的核心思想：多臂老虎机（探索-利用权衡的经典框架）、自整定调节器（在线适应参数的学习型控制器）、遗憾率最小化控制器（从长期平均代价角度优化）、以及极小极大最优双控制器（提供稳健双控制的新框架）。

Result: 前三类方法（多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、遗憾率最小化控制器）已有悠久历史和丰富文献积累，而极小极大最优双控制器代表了稳健双控制的一个有前景的新兴研究框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过综述为双控制问题的理论研究提供统一视角，并指出极小极大最优双控制器是未来值得深入探索的稳健双控制方向。

Abstract: The term "dual control" refers to the dual objective of simultaneously balancing exploration and exploitation. Problems of this kind have been studied for nearly a century. This paper is devoted to theory and methodology relevant for optimal control of linear time-invariant systems whose parameters are initially unknown and must be learned by active probing. We review the main ideas underlying four major research directions: Multi-armed bandits, self-tuning regulators, regret rate minimizing controllers, and minimax optimal dual controllers. The first three have a long history and rich literature, whereas the fourth provides a promising framework for robust dual control.

</details>


### [154] [Nonlinear Controllability and the Propagation of Local Information: From the Kalman Family to Lie Brackets, Rotation Groups, and Reachable Subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20094)
*Philippe Mullhaupt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了一条基于Lie理论的路线，从线性可控性推广到矩阵Lie群上的非线性可控性，通过Lie括号、指数映射、Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式等工具，重点研究旋转群SO(n)上的可控性传播机制，并对比了Sussmann的一般非线性局部可控性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是探究线性可控性中的局部代数信息能否被传播为可达集的全局陈述。线性情形中矩阵指数、Cayley-Hamilton定理和Kalman族将可控性归约为有限维线性代数；但非线性情形中Lie代数可能无限维，有限维Lie对应关系可能失效，因此需要发展一条自洽的Lie理论路线来弥合这一差距。

Method: 文章采用Lie理论方法：以矩阵Lie群为框架，通过斜对称矩阵、指数映射、交换子、单参数子群及Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式作为具体工具，在SO(n)上展示无穷小方向如何传播。具体包括：数值模拟SO(3)上的受控旋转几何；给出SO(4)和SO(7)的显式交换子序列可视化局部控制方向的传播；发展右不变系统的可达子群论证，提供Yamabe步之后的详细证明序列，并对上三角矩阵代数给出可解、幂零和理想子代数示例。

Result: 文章建立了从线性可控性的有限维线性代数化归到非线性矩阵Lie群可控性的完整理论路径，通过旋转群SO(n)的具体实例验证了Lie括号驱动的可控性传播机制，并以上三角矩阵代数中的可解、幂零和理想结构示例以及Yamabe步的证明序列支撑了可达子群论证。

Conclusion: 有限维Lie机制与Sussmann的一般非线性局部可控性理论之间存在实质性对照：前者的代数刚性在矩阵Lie群结构下仍能给出清晰的可控性刻画，而后者覆盖更广但缺乏同等程度的代数显式性。旋转群的情形充分展示了无穷小代数信息如何具体地、可计算地传播为可达集性质。

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [155] [Worst-Case Probability Bounds for Finite-Horizon Safety under Moment Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20121)
*Renato Loureiro,Torbjørn Cunis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时间范围内动力系统进入不希望区域的上界概率估计问题，提出基于矩-SOS（moment-SOS）框架的测度规划及其松弛解法，并引入与经典屏障函数结构相似但对偶形式产生概率性证书的SOS程序。通过包括轨道物体在内的多个案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统的安全性认证在实际中至关重要，但系统初始状态往往仅已知有限阶矩或处于某个区间内，这使得精确概率计算困难。现有屏障函数方法主要用于确定性安全性证明，缺乏处理概率性安全证书的工具。

Method: 将问题形式化为基于测度的规划，利用矩-SOS（moment-SOS）框架进行松弛求解；同时引入相应的对偶函数规划，并将其加强为SOS程序。该对偶形式在结构上与经典屏障函数技术相似，但输出的是概率性安全证书。

Result: 通过多个案例研究验证了所提方法的有效性，其中包括一个轨道物体的案例，展示了方法在实际复杂场景中的适用性和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的测度规划与moment-SOS松弛方法能够为有限时间动力系统在不确定初始状态下的安全性提供概率性上界估计，且其与屏障函数的结构关联为概率安全认证提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of estimating upper bounds on the probability that a dynamical system will enter an undesirable region at some point within a finite time horizon. The primary source of uncertainty lies in the system's initial state, for which only a finite set of moments is known or within a prescribed interval. To tackle this problem, we formulate a measure-based program and propose its relaxation using the moment-sum-of-squares (moment-SOS) framework. The corresponding dual problem is introduced as a functional program, which is subsequently strengthened into a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through multiple case studies, including a case involving an object in orbit.

</details>


### [156] [Dynamic Portfolio Optimization under CVaR Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20179)
*Anran Hu,Silvana M. Pesenti,Xiaofei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了终端损失在CVaR约束下的连续时间动态投资组合优化问题。利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示，在无需市场完备性的条件下，对一般凸交易目标建立了最优策略的存在性和强对偶性。基于此提出对偶嵌套二分-黄金搜索算法，内层迭代归结为标准无约束随机控制问题，并证明了策略随迭代收敛到最优控制。数值实验表明，约束非紧时恢复Merton策略；约束紧时最优策略呈状态依赖，投资者在不利结果后降低风险敞口，在有利结果后保持甚至临近到期时增加敞口，形成跨状态的非对称再配置。非流通禀赋风险放大保守调整，而价格冲击降低目标持仓和调整速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统投资组合优化通常使用VaR或CVaR作为风险约束，但连续时间下带CVaR约束的动态优化往往依赖市场完备性假设且难以求解。本文旨在针对一般凸交易目标，在不要求市场完备性的前提下，研究终端CVaR约束下最优策略的存在性、对偶结构以及可计算的数值算法。

Method: 利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示建立强对偶性和最优策略存在性；提出基于对偶的嵌套二分-黄金搜索算法，在外层对阈值和拉格朗日乘子进行搜索，内层迭代归结为标准无约束随机控制问题；并证明迭代策略在迭代次数趋于无穷时收敛到最优控制。

Result: 数值实验验证：约束非紧时恢复经典Merton策略；约束紧时最优策略呈状态依赖，不利结果后降低风险敞口，有利结果后保持甚至临近到期时增加敞口，体现跨状态非对称再配置。非流通禀赋风险放大保守调整程度，价格冲击降低目标持仓及调整速度。

Conclusion: 终端CVaR约束产生的不是均匀去风险，而是跨状态的非对称资产再配置。所提出的对偶算法在一般凸目标和市场不完备情形下均有效，为动态CVaR约束下的投资组合优化提供了理论保障与可行算法。

Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. We prove that the resulting strategies converge to the optimal control as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Numerical experiments recover the Merton policy when the risk constraint is nonbinding. When the constraint is binding, the optimal strategy becomes state dependent: the investor reduces risky exposure following adverse outcomes but preserves, and near maturity may increase, exposure following favorable outcomes. Thus, a terminal CVaR constraint produces an asymmetric reallocation across states rather than uniform de-risking. Nontraded endowment risk amplifies the conservative adjustment, whereas price impact lowers desired positions and adjustment speeds.

</details>


### [157] [Direct vs. Indirect Data-Driven Control: Case-study of Switching Systems Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20207)
*Alexis Vuille,Guillaume O. Berger,Raphaël M. Jungers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于间接方法（先系统辨识后控制）的切换线性系统稳定性分析框架，结合机器学习泛化界、系统辨识和二次Lyapunov分析的敏感性分析，提供了有限样本收敛率保证，并通过数值实验表明在中等至高噪声水平下间接方法比直接方法具有更紧的概率保证和更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制中直接方法已有针对切换线性系统的有限样本稳定性分析保证，但间接方法的类似保证仍然缺失。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为间接方法提供有限样本保证，并与直接方法进行公平比较。

Method: 结合三类工具：（1）机器学习的泛化界理论；（2）系统辨识的有限样本结果；（3）二次Lyapunov分析的敏感性分析。同时扩展了现有直接数据驱动方法来处理测量噪声（超越现有的有界噪声假设）。

Result: 间接方法框架在含噪状态测量下为切换线性系统提供了有限样本收敛率保证；在中等至高噪声水平下，间接方法比直接方法产生更紧的概率保证，并对噪声和异常值具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析与数值实验相结合，表明基于间接方法的稳定性分析在噪声环境下优于直接方法，为数据驱动控制的方法论选择提供了重要的理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: A central methodological question in data-driven control is whether to adopt a direct or indirect approach. Direct methods infer a controller or certificate directly from data, while indirect methods first identify a system model and then apply model-based control techniques. Recent developments of the direct method have led to finite-sample guarantees for the data-driven stability analysis of switched linear systems under various settings. However, for the indirect method, such guarantees remain largely elusive. In this paper, we provide a novel framework for the stability analysis of switched linear systems from noisy state measurements, using the indirect approach and quadratic Lyapunov analysis. Our framework comes with finite-sample guarantees on the convergence rate of the system. For that, we combine generalization bounds from machine learning and system identification with sensitivity analysis from quadratic Lyapunov analysis. To enable comparison, we also extend existing direct data-driven methods to handle measurement noise beyond the bounded noise case currently available in the literature. Finally, we compare the two approaches through numerical experiments, revealing that under moderate-to-high noise levels the indirect approach yields tighter probabilistic guarantees as well as greater robustness to noise and outliers than the direct approach

</details>


### [158] [Directional Subdifferentials of the Value Function in Asplund Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20241)
*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Asplund空间中建立了值函数的方向性次微分变分框架，引入新的方向性条件以解决无穷维空间中缺乏紧致性的问题，并推导出值函数方向性极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的值函数方向性次微分结果主要限于有限维设置，而许多实际优化与灵敏度分析问题需要在无穷维空间（如Asplund空间）中处理。无穷维空间中紧致性的缺失使得现有方法无法直接推广，亟需一个全面的变分框架来支撑无穷维层次系统的灵敏度分析。

Method: 在Asplund空间中建立方向性次微分的变分框架，建立方向性非光滑分析的关键演算规则以扩展到无穷维。针对无穷维空间中紧致性的缺失，引入一个新的方向性条件（directional condition），在此条件下推导值函数的方向性极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计。

Result: 建立了Asplund空间中值函数方向性次微分的关键演算规则，并通过所提出的方向性条件，成功获得了值函数方向性极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计，弥补了无穷维空间中因缺乏紧致性导致的分析困难。

Conclusion: 该工作为无穷维层次系统的灵敏度分析提供了更为精细的分析基础，将方向性非光滑分析从有限维推广至无穷维Asplund空间，丰富了值函数扰动响应的定量度量理论。

Abstract: Directional subdifferentials of the value function provide a quantitative measure of optimal value response to perturbations. While existing results are largely limited to finite-dimensional settings, this paper develops a comprehensive variational framework in Asplund spaces. We establish essential directional calculus rules, extending directional nonsmooth analysis to infinite dimensions. To address the lack of compactness of bounded sets in infinite-dimensional spaces, we introduce a new directional condition, under which we derive upper estimates for directional limiting and singular subdifferentials of the value function. These results provide a refined analytical foundation for sensitivity analysis in infinite-dimensional hierarchical systems.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [The near-wall cycle for skin-friction generation revealed through explainable deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19213)
*Andres Cremades,Sergio Hoyas,Ricardo Vinuesa*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用神经网络预测湍流槽道流中的未来速度场和壁面剪应力分布，并通过SHAP重要性图识别对流场预测最关键的输入区域，发现壁面摩擦产生的主导事件呈现为成对结构（上游速度相关区域与下游摩擦相关区域），追踪这些配对结构揭示出壁面剪切应力产生的循环生命周期。该研究重新定义了近壁相干结构——不再依据流动外观，而是依据其对壁面剪应力的实际作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 壁面有界湍流中的壁面摩擦由间歇性的近壁运动产生，但传统的相干结构定义无法识别具体哪些单独事件真正产生了壁面剪切应力（从而产生摩擦）。作者希望基于因果/功能视角而非形态学视角来定义相干结构。

Method: 训练神经网络预测湍流槽道流的未来速度场和壁面剪应力分布；利用SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）生成重要性图，识别对每个预测影响最大的输入区域，从而定位关键事件并追踪其演化过程。

Result: 主导事件呈现为配对结构：上游存在一个面向壁面的高动量运动相关的速度关键区域，下游存在一个标记增强壁面剪切应力遗留的摩擦关键区域。追踪这些配对揭示了一个包含生长、流向拉伸、衰减以及偶尔分裂成新壁面剪切产生事件的循环。

Conclusion: 该研究重新定义了近壁相干结构——不是根据流动"看起来像什么"，而是根据它们"对壁面剪应力做了什么"，即基于功能/因果视角对湍流近壁结构进行新的分类和理解。

Abstract: Skin friction in wall-bounded turbulence is produced by intermittent near-wall motions, yet conventional coherent-structure definitions do not identify which individual events generate wall-shear stress, and thus friction. We train neural networks to predict the future velocity field and wall-shear-stress distribution in turbulent channel flow, and use SHAP-derived importance maps to identify the the input regions most influential for each prediction. The dominant events appear as paired objects: an upstream velocity-relevant region is associated with high-momentum motion toward the wall, while a downstream friction-relevant region marks the enhanced wall-shear-stress left in its wake. Tracking these pairs reveals a recurrent cycle of growth, streamwise elongation, decay, and occasional splitting into new wall-shear-producing events. These results redefine near-wall coherent structures not by what the flow looks like, but by what they do to wall-shear stress.

</details>


### [160] [Generalised Perturbed Convective Wave Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19236)
*Stefan Schoder,Eman Bagheri,Hugo Vincent,Thomas Brunner*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将对流扰动波动方程(cPCWE)推广到空间变化的平均密度场，得到一个用于描述运动非均匀介质中声波产生与传播的精确标量重构方程。相关工作流中的中间变量与Kovasnay的涡量、熵和声学模态相关联。在M=0.2至M=0.4范围内，通过与二维等温混合层的直接数值模拟(DNS)及Lighthill类比对比验证了精度：M>=0.25时声功率级一致性在0.9dB内，M>=0.3时在0.5dB内；M=0.2时cPCWE偏差仅-1.2dB，优于Lighthill类比的2.8dB过预测。此外，cPCWE还能解析剪切区内部的声场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的cPCWE理论仅适用于恒定平均密度场的可压缩流动，尚未推广到空间变化平均密度场的一般非均匀介质情形。作者旨在扩展该理论，提供一种能够准确描述运动非均匀介质中声波产生与传播的标量方程，并对其物理含义和各处理步骤的中间变量进行解释。

Method: 将cPCWE推广到空间变化平均密度场，推导出精确的标量重构方程。工作流依次求解Helmholtz分解问题、Poisson方程和cPCWE，并将这些中间变量与Kovasnay分解的涡量、熵和声学模态建立对应关系。通过二维等温混合层的完全可压缩DNS以及在同一框架下计算的Lighthill类比进行定量对比验证。

Result: 在M=0.2到M=0.4范围内，辐射声功率跨越多个数量级。M>=0.25时三种方法的声功率级一致性在0.9dB内，M>=0.3时在0.5dB内。在M=0.2的弱声学涨落条件下，Lighthill类比过预测2.8dB，而cPCWE仅偏差-1.2dB。cPCWE还能解决剪切区内部被涡量涨落掩盖的声场分辨率问题。

Conclusion: cPCWE在低马赫数弱声学条件下比Lighthill类比更准确，因为其源项局限在涡对合并区域，而对流和折射效应由对流波算子表示。该理论不仅能在远场准确再现辐射声，还能解析剪切区内被涡量涨落掩盖的声学场，为运动非均匀介质中的气动声学分析提供了更优的标量方程框架。

Abstract: The theory of the perturbed convective wave equation for compressible flows (cPCWE) is generalised to spatially varying mean-density fields. The resulting equation is an exact scalar reformulation of the acoustic perturbation equations and describes sound generation and propagation in moving inhomogeneous media using a single unknown. The intermediate variables of the associated workflow, in which a Helmholtz decomposition problem, a Poisson equation and the cPCWE are solved successively, are related to the vortical, entropy and acoustic modes of Kovasznay, providing a physical interpretation of each processing step. The quantitative accuracy is assessed against fully compressible direct numerical simulations (DNS) of two-dimensional isothermal mixing layer and Lighthill's analogy computed in the same framework at Mach numbers between M=0.2 and M=0.4, based on the velocity difference across the layer and the ambient speed of sound. Over this range of Mach numbers, the radiated power spans several orders of magnitude. For M>=0.25, the sound power levels obtained using the three methods agree within 0.9dB, and within 0.5dB for M>=0.3. At M=0.2, where the acoustic fluctuations are weakest relative to the hydrodynamic ones, Lighthill's analogy over-predicts the radiated power by 2.8dB. In contrast, the cPCWE deviates from the DNS reference by only -1.2dB. This closer agreement is because the cPCWE source term is confined to the vortex-pairing region, while convection and refraction are represented by its convective wave operator. Beyond reproducing the far-field sound, the cPCWE resolves the acoustic field within the shear zone itself, where the DNS' fields are masked by vortical fluctuations.

</details>


### [161] [Weakly nonlinear internal waves by tidal flow over a ridge in a shear current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19237)
*Xun Huang*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将Thorpe逐次逼近展开推广至Lamb与Dunphy所研究的受迫潮汐锁定内波生成问题。在二阶地形陡度下，每个模态通过闭式对角模态核强迫一个束缚二次谐波，但该核在均匀流中消失，退化为Thorpe极限；在三阶下，共振基波的适定性给出非线性波数修正（即Thorpe相速度移动的受迫对应物）。在Lamb与Dunphy参数下计算显示修正虽小但具有系统性，下游模态的束缚谐波使位移廓线产生适度畸变并集中于表层剪切层；修正峰值出现在模态2–3，随脊高和流强平方增长，使每个模态产生非线性减速，从理论内部解释了为何线性离散谱模型与全非线性模拟吻合良好。当分层与剪切层同位（斜温层情形）时，非线性修正显著放大，表明均匀分层结论不具有普适性，修正受N²相对剪切层位置的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性效应对受迫内波离散模态谱的修正机制，并解释线性离散谱模型为何与全非线性模拟高度一致，同时检验该结论在不同分层条件下的普适性。

Method: 将Thorpe的逐次逼近展开方法推广至存在稳定剪切流的受迫潮汐锁定内波生成问题。在二阶地形陡度下推导闭式对角模态核以得到束缚二次谐波；在三阶下通过共振基波的适定性条件导出非线性波数修正。

Result: 发现非线性修正随脊高和流强的平方增长，峰值位于模态2–3，对所有模态表现为非线性减速。斜温层（分层与剪切同位）情形会大幅放大修正，而均匀分层情形修正较小。

Conclusion: 非线性的主要效应是对每个模态做小幅系统性减速及有限幅畸变，这解释了线性模型与全非线性模拟的吻合；但该结论仅对均匀分层成立，当N²与剪切层相对位置变化时非线性修正显著增强，表明需依据具体海洋层结条件评估线性模型的适用性。

Abstract: We extend Thorpe successive approximation expansion to the forced, tide locked internal wave generation problem of Lamb and Dunphy, where a barotropic tide over a ridge radiates a discrete spectrum of Taylor Goldstein eigenmodes in a steady shear current. At second order in topographic steepness, each mode forces a bound second harmonic via diagonal mode pair kernels derived in closed form; both kernels vanish for uniform flow, recovering Thorpe limit. At third order, solvability of the resonant fundamental yields a nonlinear wavenumber correctionthe forced counterpart of Thorpe phase speed shift, with tidal frequency fixed. Computations at the parameters of Lamb and Dunphy show that the corrections are small but systematic, with the bound harmonic distorting the displacement profile modestly and concentrated in the surface shear layer for downstream modes. The corrections peak at modes 2 to 3, grow quadratically with ridge height and current strength, and amount to a nonlinear slow-down of every mode, explaining from within the theory why the linear discrete spectrum model agreed so well with fully nonlinear simulations. A pycnocline case with stratification colocated with the shear layer amplifies the nonlinear corrections substantially, showing that the uniform stratification verdict is not generic and that the corrections are controlled by where N2 sits relative to the shear.

</details>


### [162] [Modeling the compressible flow field of an impulsively started circular cylinder with refined potential flow theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19246)
*Taofiq Omoniyi Amoloye,Leke Thaddeus Oladimeji,Mahmoud A. Hayajnh,Olalekan Adebayo Olayemi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的解析模型，能够以比完整数值模拟更低的计算成本，预测高速流体绕圆柱流动时的尾流形态、湍流和激波特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速流体绕圆柱流动涉及复杂的尾流、湍流和激波现象，完整数值模拟计算成本高昂，需要更高效的预测方法。

Method: 建立新的解析模型，在降低计算量的同时捕捉绕圆柱高速流动中的尾流形态、湍流和激波等关键物理特征。

Result: 该解析模型成功预测了高速绕圆柱流动的尾流、湍流和激波现象，且计算开销显著低于完整数值模拟。

Conclusion: 该解析模型为高速流动问题提供了一种高效替代方案，兼顾准确性与计算效率。

Abstract: New analytical model predicts how fluid flows around a cylinder at high speeds, capturing wake patterns, turbulence, and shock waves with less computation than full simulations.

</details>


### [163] [A Sharp and Conservative VOF Method for Multicomponent Liquid--Gas Mass Transfer: Bubble Dissolution and Droplet Evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19254)
*Shuo Zhao,Jie Zhang,Ming-Jiu Ni*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种尖锐且保守的几何VOF-有限体积方法，用于多组分液-气传质问题。该方法在重建界面上直接施加单侧Robin条件和双侧温度通量跳跃条件，避免了体积正则化和显式时间耦合，并通过顺序耦合策略求解各相物种及温度方程，实现了对变形界面处多组分传质的高精度模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组分液-气传质涉及界面上的质量平衡、潜热交换和汽液平衡，这些过程相互耦合且界面强变形。传统方法要么采用体积正则化产生界面模糊，要么依赖上一时间步的界面数据进行显式耦合，导致精度和守恒性受限。因此需要一种在几何上完全尖锐、无需显式时间耦合的VOF方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出双场标量输运处理：物种和温度方程在液、气相中分别求解；通过嵌入边界离散化在重建界面上直接施加单向Robin边界条件和双向温度通量跳跃条件。采用一致的几何平流格式同时输运体积、动量、能量和物种；开发顺序耦合策略依次确定界面部分质量通量、封闭温度方程并更新热力学平衡态。

Result: 通过单/多组分气泡溶解、单组分液滴蒸发、非理想乙醇-异辛烷液滴蒸发以及坐滴水-甘油蒸发等算例验证，结果表明该方法具有二阶精度、准确的界面通量预测、良好的质量和能量守恒性，并能捕捉气体置换、共沸挥发性反转以及组分驱动的Marangoni流动等复杂多组分效应。

Conclusion: 该几何VOF-有限体积方法为实现尖锐的多组分液-气界面传质模拟提供了一条有效途径，在精度、守恒性和处理复杂多组分物理现象方面均表现优异。

Abstract: We present a sharp and conservative geometrical VOF--finite-volume method for multicomponent liquid--gas mass transfer across deformable interfaces. The method solves problems in which multiple species are coupled through interfacial mass balances, latent-heat exchange, and vapor--liquid equilibrium. The key novelty is a fully sharp two-field treatment of scalar transport: the species and temperature equations are solved separately in the liquid and gas phases, while the one-sided Robin conditions for species and the two-sided flux jump for temperature are imposed directly on the reconstructed interface through an embedded-boundary discretization. This avoids both volumetric regularization of interfacial source terms and explicit coupling based on previous-time-step interfacial data. A consistent geometrical advection scheme is used for volume, momentum, energy, and species transport, and a sequential coupling strategy is developed to determine the partial interfacial mass fluxes, close the temperature equation, and update the thermodynamic-equilibrium state. The method is validated through single- and multicomponent bubble dissolution, single-component droplet evaporation, non-ideal ethanol--isooctane droplet evaporation, and sessile water--glycerol droplet evaporation. The results demonstrate second-order accuracy, accurate interfacial flux prediction, good mass and energy conservation, and the ability to capture complex multicomponent effects such as gas replacement, azeotropic volatility reversal, and composition-driven Marangoni flow.

</details>


### [164] [Effect of Microscale Turbulent Structures Dynamics on Forced Convection in Turbulent Porous Media Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19342)
*Ching-Wei Huang,Vishal Srikanth,Andrey V. Kuznetsov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过大涡模拟（LES）在雷诺数300条件下研究了多孔介质中湍流对流传热，发现微尺度涡（小于孔径的流动结构）对传热有显著影响，且传热特性取决于微涡是附着还是脱离障碍物表面。湍流强度与振铃数密度相关，障碍物形状（方形/圆形）和孔隙率（0.50/0.87）影响主导传热模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前尚未有人研究多孔介质中微尺度流动结构（小于孔径）对湍流传热的影响，该研究旨在填补这一空白，通过确定微涡流对湍流多孔介质流动中对流传热的影响，为优化多孔介质几何结构提供理论依据。

Method: 采用大涡模拟（LES）在雷诺数300条件下对均质多孔介质中的湍流进行数值模拟，并对比障碍物为方形和圆形圆柱、孔隙率分别为0.50和0.87的情况，分析微涡的附着/脱离状态与Nusselt数和压力不稳定性之间的光谱相关性。

Result: 发现对流传热特性取决于微涡是附着还是脱离障碍物表面；Nusselt数与涡脱落引起的压力不稳定性之间存在光谱相关性；收敛通道间周期性形成的高压区域引发二次流动不稳定性，产生局部逆压梯度，影响流速和对流传热。

Conclusion: 理解影响对流传热的主导模态有助于找到多孔介质的最优几何结构，为多孔介质传热系统的设计与优化提供指导。

Abstract: The influence of microscale flow structures (smaller than the pore size) on turbulent heat transfer in porous media has not been yet investigated. The goal of this study is to determine the influence of the micro-vortices on convection heat transfer in turbulent porous media flow. Turbulent flow in a homogeneous porous medium was investigated using Large Eddy Simulation (LES) at a Reynolds number of 300. We observed that the convection heat transfer characteristics are dependent on whether the micro-vortices are attached or detached from the surface of the obstacle. There is a spectral correlation between the Nusselt number and the pressure instabilities due to vortex shedding. A secondary flow instability occurs due to high pressure regions forming periodically near the converging pathway between obstacles. This causes local adverse pressure gradient, affecting the flow velocity and convection heat transfer. This study has been performed for obstacles with shapes of square and circular cylinders at porosities of 0.50 and 0.87. Understanding the dominant modes that affect convection heat transfer can aid in finding an optimum geometry for the porous medium.

</details>


### [165] [Stability and nonlinear dynamics of three-layer viscous films inside a vertical cylindrical tube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19417)
*David Halpern,Awa Traore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究三层不互溶粘性液体在竖直圆柱管内壁上的流动动力学与稳定性，通过长波渐近分析、线性稳定性分析和非线性模拟揭示了三种动力学结局，并发现中层液体破裂这一三层构型独有的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该构型与分层芯-环输送过程相关，理解三层界面系统的动力学与稳定性对于工业输送过程的设计与控制至关重要。与单界面或双界面圆柱膜流不同，三层构型存在更复杂的界面耦合现象，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用长波渐近分析推导出三个界面运动的三重耦合非线性演化方程组；通过线性稳定性分析预测失稳模式；利用非线性数值模拟考察动力学演化；使用数值延拓法计算行波解分支及其极限点。

Result: 线性分析揭示了一种持久的界面长波不稳定性（空气-芯界面的毛细/瑞利-普拉托不稳定性），以及在某些参数区域内因界面耦合产生的二次有限波数不稳定带。非线性模拟识别出三种动力学结局：有限振幅行波饱和、空气芯塞堵闭合、以及中层液体破裂而空气芯保持开放。数值延拓得到行波分支，其对饱和波向塞堵转变的预测与时间模拟吻合，但无法捕捉中层塌陷破裂机制。

Conclusion: 三层构型引入的中层液膜破裂机制是单界面或双界面圆柱膜流所不具备的新现象，表明传统行波分析在完全描述三层系统动力学方面存在局限。稳定性特征对厚度、粘度比和表面张力参数高度敏感，并伴随色散关系中竞争最大值的模式切换现象。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics and stability of three immiscible viscous liquid layers coating the interior of a vertical cylindrical tube, a configuration relevant to stratified core--annular transport processes. A long-wave asymptotic analysis yields a coupled system of nonlinear evolution equations governing the motion of the three interfaces. Linear stability analysis predicts a persistent long-wave instability, the capillary (Rayleigh--Plateau) instability of the air--core interface, together with secondary finite-wavenumber instability bands that emerge from interfacial coupling in certain parameter regimes. These stability characteristics depend sensitively on the layer thicknesses, viscosity ratios, and surface tension parameters, and include mode-switching associated with competing maxima in the dispersion relation. Nonlinear simulations reveal three distinct dynamical outcomes: saturation to finite-amplitude travelling waves, air-core closure through plug formation, and rupture of the intermediate liquid layer while the air core remains open. The intermediate-layer rupture mechanism is unique to the three-layer configuration which has no analogue in one- or two-interface cylindrical film flows. Numerical continuation is used to compute branches of travelling-wave solutions and their associated limit points. Comparison with time-dependent simulations shows that travelling-wave branches successfully predict the transition from saturated waves to plug formation, but do not capture the distinct rupture mechanism associated with collapse of the intermediate layer.

</details>
